**וְהַאֲרַכְתֶּם יָמִים בָּאָרֶץ אֲשֶׁר תִּירָשׁוּן.** וְשֶׁתִּקְנוּ אוֹתוֹ אֲרִיכוּת יָמִים לְעוֹלָם שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ אָרוֹךְ, בִּהְיוֹתְכֶם בָּאָרֶץ בְּלִי צַעַר וּבְאֵין מַחֲרִיד וּמוֹנֵעַ.


והארכתם ימים בארץ אשר תירשון, so that you will acquire “long life” in a world which is itself one of unlimited duration.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:1:1)


**וְזֹאת הַמִּצְוָה.** אָמְנָם הַמִּצְוֹת הַתְּלוּיוֹת בָּאָרֶץ וּבִכְנִיסַתְכֶם בָּהּ, כִּוֵּן בָּהֶם הַמְצַוֶּה יִתְבָּרַךְ.


וזאת המצוה, so called because the commandments that can be fulfilled only in the land of Israel are the ones through whose performance the One Who has commanded them considers Himself blessed.



### Verse 2

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:2:1)


**לְמַעַן תִּירָא אֶת ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ.** שֶׁתִּזְכֹּר שֶׁהוּא בַּעַל הַקַּרְקַע, וְכִי גֵּרִים וְתוֹשָׁבִים אַתֶּם עִמּוֹ (ויקרא כה: כג).


למען תירא את ה' אלוקיך, for you will remember then that He is the owner of the earth you till, and that you are considered as aliens, or at best as resident strangers by Him.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:2:2)


**אַתָּה וּבִנְךָ וּבֶן בִּנְךָ.** שֶׁגַּם הַדּוֹרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא רָאוּ אֶת מַעֲשֵׂה ה׳ הַגָּדוֹל יִירָאוּהוּ, כְּשֶׁיְּקַבְּלוּ אֵלֶּה הַמִּצְווֹת מִכֶּם שֶׁרְאִיתֶם.


אתה, ובנך, ובן בנך. This is a reminder that even the generations who had not been eyewitnesses to G’ds powerful miracles will learn to revere Him when they accept the commandments, as you have seen.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:2:3)


**וּלְמַעַן יַאֲרִיכֻן יָמֶיךָ.** וּכְדֵי שֶׁאֵלּוּ הַמִּצְוֹת יִקְנוּ לְךָ חַיֵּי עוֹלָם שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ אָרֹךְ.


ולמען יאריכון ימיך, and in order that these commandments (their performance) acquire for you your ticket to the world after death, a world in which there is only long, infinite life.



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:3:1)


**וְשָׁמַעְתָּ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** וּבִהְיוֹת זֶה הַתַּכְלִית הַמְכֻוָּן מֵאֵת הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ בְּרֹב חַסְדּוֹ עָלֶיךָ, רָאוּי שְׁתִּשְׁמַע וְתָבִין אֶת כַּוָּנַת הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ בְּכָל אֵלֶּה.


ושמעת ישראל, when you have attained this purpose which G’d had had in mind for you from the beginning in His great kindness. It is important that through performance and awareness you will appreciate the tremendous goodwill G’d has demonstrated by His legislating these commandments.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:3:2)


**וְשָׁמַרְתָּ לַעֲשׂוֹת אֲשֶׁר יִיטַב לְךָ.** וְתִשְׁתַּדֵּל לַעֲשׂוֹת אָז מֵאַהֲבָה לְרָצוֹן לְפָנָיו מַה שֶּׁיִּיטַב לְךָ לְהַקְנוֹתְךָ אֹשֶׁר נִצְחִי, וּכְמוֹ כֵן תִּשְׁמוֹר לַעֲשׂוֹת אַתָּה שֶׁתִּרְבּוּן מְאֹד, וְזֶה בִּשְׁמִירַת צְדָקָה וּמִשְׁפָּט לִכְבוֹדוֹ.


ושמרת לעשות אשר ייטב לך, and you will endeavour to carry out all these tasks out of a feeling of love for G’d, in order to be worthy of His goodwill, aware that He is interested in creating conditions which will make you merit a continued existence. Seeing that this is His purpose in legislating any and all of these commandments, you will also observe these commandments in order to multiply greatly on earth. This is a goal you will achieve if your performance of these commandments is not for the sake of the material reward but לשמה, for His honour, as acts of righteousness and justice in the pure sense. כאשר דבר ה' אלוקיך לך, ארץ זבת חלב ודבש, as He had said to you concerning this land that it “flows with milk and honey.” This land is inherently capable of providing those of its owners who take the lesson we just spelled out to heart, with an easy livelihood, a livelihood which leaves the person sufficient leisure time to pursue Torah studies and by means of this to penetrate more deeply into its meaning and also to be fruitful and to multiply.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:3:3)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר ה׳ אֱלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֶיךָ לָךְ אֶרֶץ זָבַת חָלָב וּדְבָשׁ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא דִּבֵּר לְךָ אֶרֶץ זָבַת חָלָב וּדְבָשׁ, מוּכֶנֶת לְעוֹבְדָיו לָזֶה הַתַּכְלִית, שֶׁתִּתְפַּרְנְסוּ בָּהּ שֶׁלֹּא בְּצַעַר, וְיִהְיֶה לָכֶם פְּנַאי לְעִיּוּן וּלְמַעֲשֶׂה, לִפְרוֹת וְלִרְבּוֹת.



### Verse 4

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4:1)


**שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל.** הִתְבּוֹנֵן וְהָבֵן זֶה.


שמע ישראל, listen Israel with your mind open;


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4:2)


**ה׳.** הַנּוֹתֵן מְצִיאוּת כָּל נִבְרָא.


'ה, He is the One Who is responsible for the existence of a physical universe and our globe, the only part of it on which physical life exists.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4:3)


**אֱלֹהֵינוּ.** הוּא נִבְחַר שֶׁבַּנִּבְדָּלִים אֶצְלֵנוּ, אֲשֶׁר מִמֶּנּוּ נְקַוֶּה לְהַשִּׂיג חֲפָצֵינוּ בְּלִי אֶמְצָעִי. וּמִצַּד הֱיוֹתוֹ עֶלְיוֹן בְּכֹחוֹ הַבּוֹרֵא, הִנֵּה לוֹ לְבַדּוֹ רָאוּי לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת. וּמִצַּד שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ תִּקְוָתֵנוּ בְּלִי אֶמְצָעִי, רָאוּי שֶׁאֵלָיו לְבַדּוֹ נִתְפַּלֵּל וְנַעֲבוֹד.


אלוקינו, He is the choicest of all the abstract forces assigned to supervise the conduct of human beings, and even inert creatures. From Him we yearn for assistance in helping us to achieve our purpose on earth. We do not turn to any intermediary. He alone is fit to have prayers addressed to.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4:4)


**ה׳ אֶחָד.** וּבִהְיוֹתוֹ נוֹתֵן הַמְּצִיאוּת אַחַר אֲפִיסוּת מֻחְלָט, הִתְבָּאֵר שֶׁאֵין נִמְצָא זוּלָתוֹ מִמִּין מְצִיאוּתוֹ, וְשֶׁהוּא נִבְדָּל בְּמִין מִמְּצִיאוּת עוֹלָם הַנִּפְסָדִים וּמִמְּצִיאוּת עוֹלָם הַגַּלְגַּלִּים וּמִמְּצִיאוּת עוֹלָם הַמַּלְאָכִים, בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁהוּא יָחִיד בְּעוֹלָמוֹ הָרְבִיעִי. וְזֶה נִרְאֶה שֶׁנִּרְמַז בִּהְיוֹת אוֹת הַדָּלֶ״ת שֶׁל אֶחָד גְּדוֹלָה, וּבְאוֹת הָעַיִ״ן שֶׁל שְׁמַע הַגְּדוֹלָה נִרְמַז שֶׁרָאוּי לִפְקֹחַ עַיִן וּלְהַגְדִּיל הָעִיּוּן בָּזֶה. וּלְזֶה אָמְרוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (ברכות פרק היה קורא): וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיַּאֲרִיךְ בְּדָלֶ״ת שֶׁל אֶחָד, לְמַעַן יְכַוֵּן לָזֶה.


ה' אחד; seeing that He created all phenomena in this universe, celestial or terrestrial out of a total void, it is logically impossible for there to be another phenomenon representing an existence independent of Him. It also makes him basically different, unique, for all things created by Him are by definition potentially terminal, transient existences. He is absolutely unique in the world based on four basic raw materials, water, fire, earth, air. This idea appears to be hinted at in the letter ד=4 in the word אחד which is written larger than usual and the letter ע in the word שמע which is also written in larger than normal script, suggesting that we should use our eyes to take note of these concepts, the “testimony, עד” that this is so. This is why our sages said that we must draw out the word אחד when reciting it so as to give us time to reflect on the full meaning of the verse.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:5:1)


**וְאָהַבְתָּ.** תִּשְׂמַח לַעֲשׂוֹת דָּבָר שֶׁיִּיטַב בְּעֵינָיו, כַּאֲשֶׁר תָּבִין שֶׁאֵין תַּכְלִית נִכְבָּד כָּזֶה.


ואהבת, you will then enjoy doing things which are pleasing to Him when you understand that there is nothing in this world which is more worth doing. מה העדות, a reference to the philosophical, theological aspects of the Torah. These invariably relate to the supernatural matters described in the Torah.

והחקים והמשפטים, the practical laws,

**עֲבָדִים הָיִינוּ.** וּבִהְיוֹת שֶׁבְּעַבְדוּתֵנוּ לֹא הָיִינוּ יְכוֹלִים לִקְנוֹת הַשְּׁלֵמוּת הַמְּכֻוָּן מֵאִתּוֹ, הִפְלִיא לַעֲשׂוֹת לְהוֹצִיאֵנוּ וּלְהָבִיא אוֹתָנוּ אֶל הָאָרֶץ שֶׁנּוּכַל לִקְנוֹת בָּהּ אוֹתָהּ הַשְּׁלֵמוּת.


עבדים היינו, seeing that during the many years of our being slaves we could not attain the degree of perfection G’d had in mind for us to attain, He performed miracles in order to lead us out and to bring us to the land in which we could attain this degree of perfection.

ויצונו ה' לעשות את כל החקים האלה, in order to make us ready by means of their observance,

**לְיִרְאָה אֶת ה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ.** וְזֶה כְּהִתְבּוֹנֵן וְהִכִּיר אֶת גָּדְלוֹ, וְכָל זֶה רָצָה לְטוֹב לָנוּ כָּל הַיָּמִים. כִּי חָפֵץ חֶסֶד הוּא, לֹא לְתוֹעַלְתּוֹ. לְחַיּוֹתֵנוּ כְּהַיּוֹם הַזֶּה.


ליראה את ה' אלוקינו, this is the result of acquiring a measure of understanding of His greatness; all of this G’d wanted us to appreciate לטוב לנו כל הימים, for He desires to confer goodness, love, He has no egotistical motives. לחיותנו כהיום הזה. To keep us alive as of this day.



### Verse 25

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:25:1)


**וּצְדָקָה תִּהְיֶה לָּנוּ.** וְזֶה שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְךָ לְטוֹב לָנוּ כָּל הַיָּמִים, רְצוֹנִי לוֹמַר לְטוֹב לָנוּ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וּבָעוֹלָם הַבָּא.


This is what I had in mind when I said to you וצדקה תהיה לו לטוב לנו כל הימים, it will be good for us not only in this life but will be a charitable act assuring us of life in the hereafter.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 6:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/6:25:2)


**לְחַיֹּתֵנוּ כְּהַיּוֹם הַזֶּה.** חַיֵּי שָׁעָה, וְלִהְיוֹת לָנוּ צְדָקָה וּזְכוּת לִפְנֵי ה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ בָּעוֹלָם הַנִּצְחִי.


We will enjoy the transient life on this earth as a result of having first assured ourselves through observance of the commandments of our share in the world to come, i.e. eternal life.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 6

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:6:1)


**כִּי עַם קָדוֹשׁ אַתָּה.** וְאֵין רָאוּי שֶׁתְּחַלֵּל קְדֻשָּׁתְךָ לְהוֹלִיד זֶרַע פָּסוּל, כַּאֲמָרוֹ ״כִּי חִלֵּל יְהוּדָה אֶת קֹדֶשׁ ה׳ אֲשֶׁר אָהֵב וְגוֹ׳״ (מלאכי ב:יא).


כי עם קדוש אתה, and it is not appropriate for you to desecrate your sanctity through women committed to alien deities, who will bear you children that are already genetically corrupted. It is this that the prophet Maleachi 2,11 had in mind when he spoke of כי חלל יהודה קדש ה' אשר אהב ובעל בת נכר,”for Yehudah profaned what is holy to the Lord, what He desires, by sleeping with daughters of alien gods.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:6:2)


**בְּךָ בָּחַר.** וְלֹא בְּזֶרַע פָּסוּל, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (קידושין ע׳).


בך בחר, as distinct from corrupt seed; our sages referred to this concept as “the benevolent presence of G’d is present (rests on) only families who are genetically offspring of families with good Jewish pedigrees.” (Kidushin 70)

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:7:1

**לֹא מֵרֻבְּכֶם.** לֹא כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְכַּבֵּד בְּרֹב עָם.


לא מרבכם, not in order to be honoured by a multitude.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:8:1)


**כִּי מֵאַהֲבַת ה׳ אֶתְכֶם.** בִּהְיוֹתְכֶם זֶרַע אוֹהֲבָיו וְחוֹשְׁבֵי שְׁמוֹ.


כי מאהבת ה' אתכם, seeing that you are the seed of someone who loved Him and who was more familiar with His name than any member of any of the other nations.



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:9:1)


**הָאֵל הַנֶּאֱמָן.** קַיָּם עַל עִנְיָן אֶחָד, לֹא יִשְׁתַּנֶּה.


הא-ל הנאמן, Who is constant, not subject to change.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:9:2)


**שׁוֹמֵר הַבְּרִית וּמְשַׁלֵּם לְשׂוֹנְאָיו.** וְטַעַם ״רָשָׁע וְטוֹב לוֹ״ הוּא לִשְׁתֵּי סִבּוֹת: הָאַחַת הוּא זְכוּת אָבוֹת. וְהַשֵּׁנִית אֵיזֶה זְכוּת שֶׁנִּמְצָא לָרָשָׁע עַצְמוֹ, בִּלְתִּי מַגִּיעַ אֶל שֶׁיִּזְכֶּה בוֹ לְחַיֵּי עוֹלָם הַבָּא, וּמְשַׁלֵּם לוֹ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה, שֶׁאֵין הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְקַפֵּחַ שְׂכַר כָּל בְּרִיָּה.


שומר הברית ומשלם לשונאיו, the meaning of the words רשע וטוב לו, [a favourite argument of the heretics who point to the afflictions suffered by the pious and the good life enjoyed by the pious in this life. Ed.] can be understood on two levels: 1) the “good” the wicked appear to enjoy in this life is due to merits accumulated by their forefathers who had not been recompensed for these as yet by G’d. A prominent example of this is the enjoyable life lived by Ishmael who benefited by Avraham’s merits. G’d Himself referred to this in Genesis 21,13 when He said כי זרעך הוא, “because he is your seed. 2) The wicked person himself has accumulated some merits; seeing that on balance he does not qualify for life in the hereafter, G’d has to compensate him while he is on earth. This He does by letting him enjoy the transient joys available in this life to the extent his merits entitle him to.

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:11:1)


**הַיּוֹם לַעֲשׂוֹתָם.** וְלֹא תַטְרִיד מַחֲשַׁבְתְּךָ אִם לֹא תְקַבֵּל שָׂכָר מֵהֶם בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה. חסלת פרשת ואתחנן.



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:1)


**וְהָיָה עֵקֶב תִּשְׁמְעוּן.** הִנֵּה הַמֶּלֶךְ צִוָּה אֶת כָּל אֵלֶּה כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּזְכּוּ שֶׁיִּשְׁמֹר לָכֶם הַבְּרִית וְהַחֶסֶד, וְזֶה שֶׁאַתֶּם תִּשְׁמְרוּ הַיּוֹם לַעֲשׂוֹתָם מֵאַהֲבָה, שֶׁלֹּא עַל מְנָת לְקַבֵּל פְּרָס, וְהוּא בִּשְׁבִיל זֶה יִשְׁמֹר לָכֶם הַבְּרִית וְהַחֶסֶד.


והיה עקב תשמעון, Moses explains that the reason why the King, i.e. G’d, commands them to observe all these commandments is in order to be able to fulfill the promises made to their forefathers regarding them. The people were meant to observe these commandments out of a feeling of love for G’d, not because they did so in order to earn a reward. [If I understand the author correctly, the wordsואת החסד in the phrase את הברית והחסד, are to establish reciprocity of G’d’s loving care for the patriarchs, the parties to the covenant, and their descendants’ feeling of love on their part towards the Creator. Ed.]


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:2)


**אֵת הַמִּשְׁפָּטִים.** כִּי ״בְּמִשְׁפָט יַעֲמִיד אָרֶץ״ (משלי כט:ד).


את המשפטים, for the earth’s continued existence is linked to performance of justice by its inhabitants. (Proverbs 29,4)

**וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם.** זוֹ מִשְׁנָה.


ושמרתם, and study them, (according to Sifri)


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:4)


**וַעֲשִׂיתֶם אֹתָם.** וּבָזֶה הָאֹפֶן תַּעֲשׂוּ אוֹתָם אֶל נָכוֹן.


ועשיתם אותם, as a result of studying them you will perform them in accordance with halachah.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:5)


**וְשָׁמַר ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ לְךָ אֶת הַבְּרִית.** שֶׁנִּשְׁבַּע, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וַהֲקִימֹתִי אֶת בְּרִיתִי בֵּינִי וּבֵינֶךָ וּבֵין זַרְעֲךָ אַחֲרֶיךָ לְדֹרֹתָם לִבְרִית עוֹלָם לִהְיוֹת לְךָ לֵאלֹהִים וּלְזַרְעֲךָ אַחֲרֶיךָ״ (בראשית יז:ז). כִּי אָמְנָם בִּהְיוֹתוֹ לָנוּ לֵאלֹהִים בִּלְתִּי אֶמְצָעִי, יַשְׁפִּיעַ מְצִיאוּת נִצְחִי הֶהֹוֶה מִמֶּנּוּ בִּלְתִּי אֶמְצָעִי, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כִּי כָּל אֲשֶׁר יַעֲשֶׂה הָאֱלֹהִים הוּא יִהְיֶה לְעוֹלָם״ (קהלת ג:יד). אָמְנָם מְצִיאוּת הַנִּפְסָדִים הוּא מִמֶּנּוּ בְּאֶמְצָעִי בְּלִי סָפֵק.


ושמר ה' אלוקיך לך את הברית, the one He swore, when He said (Genesis 17,7) “I will give My covenant between Me and you, and between your descendants after you for all generations as an eternal covenant, to be your G’d and that of your descendants after you.” Being our G’d means that He will supervise our fates not by means of some intermediary, such as the fates which are supervised by appointees of G’d, including horoscopes, and angels assigned to each nation. Besides, anything which is done by G’d personally, without His having recourse to an intermediary, is something that endures forever. Compare Kohelet 3,14 כי כל אשר יעשה האלוקים הוא אשר יהיה לעולם,”anything which G’d does Himself is something that will endure forever.” By contrast, transient phenomena, [such as extinct species, for instance, Ed.] are proof of the fact that they were the products of intermediaries of G’d. [G’d had commanded the waters to produce the living creatures of the waters, and He had commanded the earth to produce vegetation and the mammals, etc. Water and the earth are both intermediaries. Ed.]


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12:6)


**וְאֶת הַחֶסֶד.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״אָז וְנָתַתִּי לְךָ וּלְזַרְעֲךָ אַחֲרֶיךָ אֵת אֶרֶץ מְגֻרֶיךָ״ (בראשית יז:ח). כִּי אָמְנָם הַטּוֹב הַהֹוֶה לַצַּדִּיקִים בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה הוּא חֶסֶד בִּלְבַד, לֹא גְּמוּל מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם.


ואת החסד, as He had said in Genesis 17,8 when He promised the land of Canaan to Avraham’s descendants. Such acts are acts of loving kindness and have nothing to do with the reward awaiting us as compensation for performing the commandments in the world to come. (based on the statement in Kidushin 39 that reward, in the sense of compensation, for commandments performed is not something that is due while we are on earth.)



### Verse 13

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:13:1)


**וַאֲהֵבְךָ.** בִּהְיוֹתְכֶם בָּנִים כִּדְמוּתוֹ בְּצַלְמוֹ, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (אבות ג, י) ״חֲבִיבִין וְגוֹ׳ שֶׁנִּקְרְאוּ בָּנִים לַמָּקוֹם״. וּבָזֶה יִהְיֶה נִצְחִיּוּת שֶׁל בְּרִית מְאֻשָּׁר.


ואהבך, He loved you, in the sense that you are “children,” reflecting His image; our sages (Avot 3,10) paraphrased this when they said: “Israel is beloved of the Lord,” as we know from the fact that He refers to them as בנים,”children of the Lord.” By performing the commandments the eternal nature of the covenant will be reinforced so that it is perfect.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:13:2)


**וּבֵרַכְךָ.** בְּמָמוֹן לְחַיֵּי שָׁעָה, וּבְזֶה יֵיטִיב אֶת הַחֶסֶד אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁבַּע, שֶׁתִּהְיוּ בָּאָרֶץ בְּאֹפֶן מְאֻשָּׁר.


וברכך, with material wealth, measurable in terms of money. This blessing improves the quality of the transient life on earth. By means of this blessing He insures your ability to enjoy the loving kindness of living in the land of Israel successfully, happily.



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15:1)


**כָּל חֹלִי.** אֲפִילּוּ הַמֻּרְגָּלִים לָבֹא מִצַּד הַמַּעֲרֶכֶת.


כל חולי, any manner of illness including those that are the result of climactic conditions.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15:2)


**הָרָעִים.** הַמִּתְדַּבְּקִים.


הרעים, the ones which are contagious.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15:3)


**אֲשֶׁר יָדַעְתָּ.** שֶׁלָּקוּ עַל הַיָּם, כַּמְבוֹאָר לְמַעְלָה.


אשר ידעת, that the Egyptians were smitten with at the Sea of Reeds.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15:4)


**לֹא יְשִׂימָם בָּךְ וּנְתָנָם בְּכָל שֹׂנְאֶיךָ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיִּתְּנֵם בְּשׂוֹנְאֶיךָ, לֹא יִדְבְּקוּ בָּךְ, כְּעִנְיַן ״יִפֹּל מִצִּדְּךָ אֶלֶף, אֵלֶיךָ לֹא יִגָּשׁ״ (תהלים צא:ז).


לא ישימם בך ונתנם בכל שונאך, even though these diseases will strike your enemies they will not infect you, as described in Psalms 91,7 “thousands may fall by your side….but the source of the death will not reach you.”



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:16:1)


**לֹא תָחוֹס עֵינְךָ עֲלֵיהֶם וְלֹא תַעֲבֹד אֶת אֱלֹהֵיהֶם.** לֹא תָחוֹס, וּבָזֶה הָאֹפֶן לֹא תַעֲבֹד אֶת אֱלֹהֵיהֶם, כִּי אִם תָּחוֹס יַחֲטִיאוּךָ בְּלִי סָפֵק.


לא תחוס עיניך עליהם ולא תעבוד את אלוהיהם. By not ignoring idolatry in your country by people remaining there you will ensure that no Israelites will worship such deities. If you will turn a blind eye to their practices they will undoubtedly lead you into sin.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:16:2)


**כִּי מוֹקֵשׁ הוּא לָךְ.** כִּי אָמְנָם הֱיוֹתְךָ חָס עֲלֵיהֶם יִהְיֶה לְךָ לְמוֹקֵשׁ שֶׁיְּפַתּוּךָ לַעֲבוֹד אֶת אֱלֹהֵיהֶם.


כי מוקש הוא לך, although you relate to them by protecting their freedom of worship, they will turn out to be a stumbling block for you by seducing you to join them in their religious rites.



### Verse 17

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:17:1)


**כִּי תֹאמַר אֵיכָה אוּכַל לְהוֹרִישָׁם, לֹא תִירָא מֵהֶם.** כְּשֶׁתֹּאמַר אֵיכָה אוּכַל לְהוֹרִישָׁם בִּהְיוֹתָם רַבִּים מִמֶּנִּי, לֹא תֹאמַר זֶה עַל צַד שֶׁתִּירָא מֵהֶם, אֶלָּא עַל צַד שֶׁתַּכִּיר שֶׁזֶּה הָיָה נִמְנָע לוּלֵא ה׳ שֶׁהָיָה לְךָ. וְזֶה כִּי **זָכֹר תִּזְכֹּר אֵת אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ לְפַרְעֹה וּלְכָל מִצְרָיִם**, שֶׁהָיוּ רַבִּים וַחֲזָקִים מִמְּךָ וּנְתָנָם בְּיָדְךָ וְהַאֲבַדְתָּ אֶת שְׁמָם.


כי תאמר...איכה אוכל להורישם, לא תירא מהם, for you will say that they outnumber you. Do not say this in a manner which will inspire fear in you, but reflect on the fact that this would be an argument only if the Lord your G’d had not already demonstrated that with His help you can overcome. זכור תזכור את אשר עשה ה' אלוקיך לפרעה וכל מצרים, they outnumbered you by a far greater margin than the Canaanites and they were stronger than the Canaanites.



### Verse 24

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:24:1)


**וְנָתַן מַלְכֵיהֶם בְּיָדְךָ, וְהַאֲבַדְתָּ אֶת שְׁמָם.** כִּי אָמְנָם קִיּוּם זֶרַע מְלוּכַת הָאוֹיְבִים הוּא לְשָׂטָן בְּכָל עֵת, כְּעִנְיַן הֲדַד נַעַר קָטָן שֶׁהָיָה מִזֶּרַע הַמֶּלֶךְ בֶּאֱדוֹם, וְקָם לְשָׂטָן לִשְׁלֹמֹה (מלכים א יא:יד).


ונתן מלכיהם בידך והאבדת את שמם, for allowing any of these Canaanites to survive would become an ongoing source of irritation for you, as demonstrated by the young Haddad, sole survivor of the people of Edom, who became a lifelong source of irritation (militarily) for such a powerful king as Solomon. (Kings I 11,14 and onwards)



### Verse 25

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:25:1)


**פֶּן תִּוָּקֵשׁ בּוֹ.** שֶׁלִּפְעָמִים יִקְרֶה שֶׁתַּצְלִיחַ בְּאוֹתוֹ כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב שֶׁעֲלֵיהֶם, וְתַחְשׁוֹב שֶׁהָיָה זֶה בְּכֹחַ אוֹתָה הָעֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים שֶׁהָיוּ עָלֶיהָ.


פן תוקש בו, for sometimes, especially if you are successful with the investment of silver and gold deriving from idolatrous sources, you will attribute this to the goodwill of their idols, which in turn will become a trap for you (theologically speaking).



### Verse 26

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:26:1)


**תְּתַעֲבֶנּוּ כִּי חֵרֶם הוּא.** תְּתַעֵב אוֹתוֹ הַכֶּסֶף וְהַזָּהָב שֶׁל תּוֹעֵבָה.


תתעבנו כי חרם הוא, you must treat that silver and gold as if it were an abomination;


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 7:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/7:26:2)


**כִּי חֵרֶם הוּא.** שֶׁלֹּא בִלְבַד יִקְרֶה שֶׁלֹּא תַצְלִיחַ בּוֹ, אֲבָל יַחֲרִים אֶת שְׁאָר נְכָסֶיךָ.


כי חרם הוא, not only would you not benefit by their silver and gold, but neither by their other possessions. It is out of bounds for you, it would bring harm to your other properties, the ones you acquired legally, with the approval of the Torah.



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1:1)


**כָּל הַמִּצְוָה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם תִּשְׁמְרוּן לַעֲשׂוֹת.** הִנֵּה הַמְכֻוָּן אֵצֶל הֶהָמוֹן הוּא הַשָּׂגַת הַצְלָחוֹת זְמַנִּיּוֹת הַנִּכְלָלוֹת בִּשְׁלוֹשָׁה מִינִים, וְהֵם אֹרֶךְ יָמִים וְהַבָּנִים וְהַמָּמוֹן. וְהִנֵּה בְּקִיּוּם הַמִּצְוֹת תַּשִּׂיגוּ אֶת כֻּלָּם, לְפִיכָךְ תִּשְׁמְרוּ לַעֲשׂוֹתָם.


כל המצוה אשר אנכי מצוך היום תשמרון לעשות, the objective of people worshipping idols is nothing other than their obsession with transient values which consist mostly of three categories, having children, living to a ripe old age, and success in amassing money or its equivalent. The Torah promises that by observing G’d’s commandments as spelled out in the Torah one may achieve all these three objectives. This is meant by the Torah writing: למען תחיון, when commanding the laws of the Torah G’d did not only mean to guarantee you eternal life in the hereafter, but He had in mind also successful, rewarding life on earth.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1:2)


**לְמַעַן תִּחְיוּן.** כִּי אָמְנָם הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ כְּשֶׁצִּוָּה, כִּוֵּן שֶׁבְּקִיּוּם מִצְוֹתָיו תַּשִּׂיגוּ אֹרֶךְ יָמִים, אֲפִלּוּ בְּחַיֵּי שָׁעָה.


ורביתם, you will multiply by having numerous children.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1:3)


**וּרְבִיתֶם.** שֶׁתַּשִּׂיגוּ רִבּוּי הַבָּנִים.


ובאתם וירשתם, attaining wealth and honour by inheriting the land.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1:4)


**וּבָאתֶם וִירִשְׁתֶּם.** וְשֶׁתַּשִּׂיגוּ עֹשֶׁר וְכָבוֹד בִּירֻשַּׁת הָאָרֶץ.



### Verse 2

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:2:1)


**וְזָכַרְתָּ אֶת כָּל הַדֶּרֶךְ.** שֶׁנָּתַן לְךָ לֶחֶם לֶאֱכוֹל וּבֶגֶד לִלְבּוֹשׁ שֶׁלֹּא בְּדֶרֶךְ טֶבַע.


וזכרת את כל הדרך, during which He gave you bread to eat, clothes to wear, by supernatural means.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:2:2)


**לְנַסּוֹתֶךָ.** אִם תַּעֲשֶׂה רְצוֹנוֹ בְּהַשִּׂיגְךָ לֶחֶם וְשִׂמְלָה שֶׁלֹּא בְּצַעַר.


לנסותך, to test you if, seeing that you did not have to work hard for a livelihood, you would in return carry out His will.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:2:3)


**לָדַעַת אֶת אֲשֶׁר בִּלְבָבְךָ.** שֶׁיִּהְיֶה מַה שֶּׁבְּלִבְּךָ יוֹצֵא לְפוֹעַל, כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּדַע כָּל מַלְאָךְ שֶׁבַּדִּין תִּהְיֶה מַעֲלָתְךָ יוֹתֵר מִן מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁרֵת, וְתִהְיֶה עָלֶיךָ יְדִיעָתִי הַפּוֹעֶלֶת לְטוֹב כָּרָאוּי לְנִמְצָא בְּפוֹעַל.


לדעת את אשר בלבבך, so that what you had in your mind will come to light, so that every angel will know that you had a justified claim to a higher rank in the universe than even the ministering angels.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:5:1)


**מְיַסְּרֶךָּ.** נוֹתֵן לְךָ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו מוּסָר נָאוֹת אֶל הַשְּׁלֵמוּת הַמְּכֻוָּן מֵאִתּוֹ.


מיסרך; along with the commandments He has given you, He gives you a superior moral/ethical challenge to help you achieve perfection as seen from His perspective.



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:7:1)


**אֶל אֶרֶץ טוֹבָה.** שֶׁנִּקְבְּצוּ בָּהּ מִינֵי הַטּוֹבוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יִקָּבְצוּ בִּגְלִיל זוּלָתוֹ, וְסִפֵּר חֲמֵשֶׁת מִינֵי הַטּוֹב, וּבְכָל אֶחָד מֵהֶם אָמַר ״אֶרֶץ״. הָרִאשׁוֹן הוּא **אֶרֶץ נַחֲלֵי מַיִם עֲיָנוֹת וּתְהֹמוֹת**, לֹא מֵי יְאוֹרִים וַאֲגַמִּים הָרָעִים, עַל הֵפֶךְ ״וְהַמַּיִם רָעִים וְהָאָרֶץ מְשַׁכָּלֶת״ (מלכים ב ב:יט). הַשֵּׁנִי הוּא **אֶרֶץ חִטָּה וּשְׂעֹרָה** הַצְּרִיכִים לַמָּזוֹן. הַשְּׁלִישִׁי הוּא **אֶרֶץ זֵית שֶׁמֶן וּדְבַשׁ** שֶׁהֵם מַעֲדַנֵּי מֶלֶךְ. הָרְבִיעִי **אֶרֶץ אֲשֶׁר לֹא בְמִסְכֵּנֻת תֹּאכַל בָּהּ לֶחֶם**, שֶׁיִּמָּצְאוּ בָהּ מָעוֹת בְּזוֹל, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וַתִּמָּלֵא אַרְצוֹ כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב וְאֵין קֵצֶה לְאוֹצְרֹתָיו״ (ישעיהו ב:ז). כִּי אָמְנָם יֹקֶר הַמָּעוֹת קָשֶׁה מִיֹּקֶר הַפֵּרוֹת, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (תענית יט) ״לֹא שָׁנוּ אֶלָּא עַל בַּצֹּרֶת שֶׁל פֵּרוֹת, אֲבָל עַל בַּצֹּרֶת שֶׁל מָעוֹת מַתְרִיעִים עֲלֵיהֶם מִיָּד״. הַחֲמִישִׁי הוּא **אֶרֶץ אֲשֶׁר אֲבָנֶיהָ בַרְזֶל**, שֶׁבָּהּ נִמְצָא רִבּוּי אֲבָנִים קָשׁוֹת וּבְהִירוֹת יָפוֹת וְטוֹבוֹת לְבִנְיָן.


אל ארץ טובה, a land which is the confluence of numerous good, desirable qualities not found together in other districts of the globe. These qualities are the following, the Torah prefacing each with the word ארץ again. 
1)	ארץ נחלי מים עינות ותהומות, not the waters from polluted rivers or static ponds, waters described as evil waters in Kings II 2,19.
 2) ארץ חטה ושעורה , crops which are basic as food.
 3) ארץ זית שמן ודבש, products fit for the palate of a king.
 4) ארץ אשר לא במסכנות תאכל בה לחם, a land in which money can be found cheaply, a land full of treasures as per Isaiah 2,7 “the land is full of silver and gold and there is no end to the treasures it hides.” Dearth of money is more serious than shortage of the products which can be bought with it, as we know from the Talmud Taanit 19 which declares that whereas the blowing of the shofar in supplication to G’d on account of a shortage of foodstuffs does not have to be arranged immediately, shortage of money, i.e. people not having the money to buy the available food products gives rise to immediate supplication to Gd including the sounding of the shofar at public prayer meetings. 
 5) ארץ אשר אבניה ברזל, a land whose stones contain iron, i.e. a useful metal, or whose stones are strong as iron, providing excellent building materials.



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:9:1)


**תַּחְצֹב נְחֹשֶׁת.** הוּא הַבַּרְזֶל הַטּוֹב שֶׁהוּא בָּהִיר וְנָאוֹת לְבִנְיָן וְכֵלִים.


תחצוב נחושת, you will mine copper; the Torah’s definition of “good” iron, material that is excellent for either building material, or raw material for a variety of vessels.



### Verse 10

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:10:1)


**וּבֵרַכְתָּ אֶת ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ.** לְמַעַן תִּזְכֹּר כִּי מֵאִתּוֹ הָיוּ אֵלֶּה לָךְ.


וברכת את ה' אלוקיך, the reason why the Lord blesses you in such a fashion is so that you will remember that only from Him could you have been given a land with such an abundance of advantages.

**מִצּוּר הַחַלָּמִישׁ.** שֶׁנֶּהְפַּךְ לְמַיִם, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״הַהֹפְכִי הַצּוּר אֲגַם־מָיִם״ (תהלים קיד:ח).


ומצור החלמיש, which was turned into water for your benefit. Compare Psalms 114,8: “Who has turned the rock into a pool of water, the flint stone into a fountain of water.”



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:16:1)


**לְמַעַן עַנֹּתְךָ.** שֶׁתְּקַיֵּם מֵעֳנִי, כְּדַאֲגַת מִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ פַּת בְּסַלּוֹ.


למען ענותך, so that you will keep His commandments out of a sense of poverty, as do people who have no bread in their baskets (which would give them a feeling of confidence).


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:16:2)


**וּלְמַעַן נַסּוֹתֶךָ.** אִם תַּעֲשֶׂה רְצוֹנוֹ בְּתִתּוֹ פַּרְנָסָתְךָ שֶׁלֹּא בְּצַעַר.


ולמען נסותך, to see if He will give you your livelihood without your having to perform back breaking labour if you perform His will as written in the Torah


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 8:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/8:16:3)


**לְהֵיטִיבְךָ.** יוֹתֵר מִמַּלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁרֵת, כָּרָאוּי לַמְנֻסֶּה לְפָנָיו.


להיטיבך, more so even than to the ministering angels, who, in spite of their devoted service are in a state of panicky flight before Him.



### Verse 19

והיה אם שכוח תשכח, this will happen if you attribute your success to your own efforts instead of giving credit to G’d and thanking Him for it by blessing Him.

**תֹּאבֵדוּן.** הָגוֹיִם מִשְּׁנֵי עוֹלָמוֹת.

## Chapter 9


### Verse 4

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:4:1)


**אַל תֹּאמַר בִּלְבָבְךָ בַּהֲדֹף.** כְּשֶׁתִּרְאֶה שֶׁתְּנַצְּחֵם שֶׁלֹּא כְּמִנְהַג הָעוֹלָם וְתַכִּיר שֶׁהוּא פֹּעַל אֱלֹהִי.


אל תאמר בלבבך בהדוף, when you see that you enjoy victory against all odds, so that you realise this victory has to be credited to your enjoying a heavenly assist, do not commit a terrible error.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:4:2)


אַל תִּטְעֶה לוֹמַר **בְּצִדְקָתִי הֱבִיאַנִי לָרֶשֶׁת.** וּכְדֵי שֶׁאִירַשׁ הָאָרֶץ מְהֵרָה בִּזְכוּתִי הָדַף אוֹתָם מְהֵרָה.


בצדקתי הביאני...לרשת, that G’d did all this on account of your being a model of righteousness, and that furthermore the speed with which all this occurred was on account of


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:4:3)


**וּבְרִשְׁעַת הַגּוֹיִם הָאֵלֶּה ה׳ מוֹרִישָׁם מִפָּנֶיךָ.** וְהִנֵּה הַסִּבָּה שֶׁהוּא נִלְחָם בָּהֶם הִיא בְּרִשְׁעָתָם, לֹא כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּירַשׁ אַתָּה מְהֵרָה.


ברשעת הגויים האלה ה' מורישם מפניך, that the Lord dispossessed them on account of their wickedness, because He wanted His retribution against these people to be orchestrated by your destroying them. This would be the very opposite of what David prayed for when he thought G’d was punishing him. He prayed that the judgment should not be executed by man but that he preferred to be punished by G;d directly, not by one of His surrogates, especially one of his peers. (Samuel II 24,14)



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:5:1)


**לֹא בְצִדְקָתְךָ אַתָּה בָא לָרֶשֶׁת.** כִּי אָמְנָם מַה שֶּׁאַתָּה יוֹרֵשׁ הָאָרֶץ אֵינוֹ בִזְכוּתְךָ, בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁאֵין רָאוּי שֶׁיִּלָּחֵם ה׳ לָכֶם לְמַעַן תִּירְשׁוּ מְהֵרָה.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:5:2)


**כִּי בְרִשְׁעַת הַגּוֹיִם הָאֵלֶּה ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ מוֹרִישָׁם מִפָּנֶיךָ.** שֶׁרָצָה שֶׁתִּהְיֶה נִקְמָתוֹ בָּם עַל יָדְךָ, שֶׁתַּחֲרִימֵם בַּעֲוֹנָם, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וַיֹּאמֶר הַשְׁמֵד״ (דברים לג:כז), עַל הֶפֶךְ ״וּבְיַד אָדָם אַל אֶפֹּלָה״ (שמואל ב כד:יד).


ולמען הקים, so that as a result of observing the commandments the nation would merit to inherit the land. We find another instance where, as a result of complying with a command by G’d to wipe out the family of Achav, G’d not only confirmed Jehu as king but extended his dynasty for four generations. (Kings II 10,30)


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:5:3)


**וּלְמַעַן הָקִים.** וּכְדֵי שֶׁבְּקִיּוּם מִצְוָתוֹ תִּזְכֶּה לָרֶשֶׁת אוֹתָהּ, עַל דֶּרֶךְ ״כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר בִּלְבָבִי עָשִׂיתָ לְבֵית אַחְאָב, בְּנֵי רְבִעִים יֵשְׁבוּ לְךָ עַל כִּסֵּא יִשְׂרָאֵל״ (מלכים ב י:ל). וְכָל זֶה עָשָׂה לְמַעַן הָקִים מַה שֶּׁנִּשְׁבַּע לָאֲבוֹת.



### Verse 6

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:6:1)


**כִּי עַם קְשֵׁה עֹרֶף אָתָּה.** שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָר שֶׁיִּהְיֶה צֶדֶק וְיֹשֶׁר לֵבָב עִם קְשִׁי הָעֹרֶף, כִּי אָמְנָם קְשֵׁה הָעֹרֶף הוּא הַהוֹלֵךְ אַחֲרֵי שְׁרִירוּת לִבּוֹ וּמַחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיּוֹדִיעֵהוּ אֵיזֶה מוֹרֶה צֶדֶק בִּרְאָיָה בְּרוּרָה שֶׁמַּחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ הִיא בִּלְתִּי טוֹבָה וּמְבִיאָה אֶל הַהֶפְסֵד, וְזֶה כִּי לֹא יִפְנֶה אֶל הַמּוֹרֶה כְּאִלּוּ עׇרְפּוֹ קָשֶׁה כְּגִיד בַּרְזֶל בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל לִפְנוֹת אָנָה וְאָנָה, אֲבָל יֵלֵךְ אַחֲרֵי שְׁרִירוּת לִבּוֹ כְּמֵאָז.


כי עם קשה עורף אתה, being stiff-necked and righteous simultaneously is a non starter, as these two character qualities are mutually exclusive. Being stiff-necked means to follow the dictates of one’s own heart, one’s own opinions instead of accepting the yoke of the Creator’s commandments as being supreme. Such a person will reject even theories which have been proven beyond doubt if they conflict with what he deems good or convenient for himself. The simile of a “stiff-necked” person or people described someone who appears unable to turn around and face his teacher, as his neck is too stiff.



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:7:1)


**זָכוֹר אַל תִּשְׁכַּח.** וְיָעִיד עַל הֱיוֹתְךָ עַם קְשֵׁה עֹרֶף, כִּי הִנֵּה חֲזַרְתֶּם לְהַקְצִיף פַּעַם אַחַר פַּעַם כְּכֶלֶב שָׁב עַל קֵיאוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּכָל פַּעַם רְאִיתֶם אֶת מוּסַר ה׳ אֱלֹהֵיכֶם וְאֶת גָּדְלוֹ.


זכור אל תשכח, Moses adds proof to his characterising the Jewish people as being stiff-necked by reminding them of how many times during his stewardship of the nation they had angered G’d, so that it reminds one of a dog which returns to his own excrement finding it attractive. This happened again and again although each time they had been subjected to disciplining by G’d, and they learned more about His greatness on each occasion.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:8:1)


**וּבְחֹרֵב הִקְצַפְתֶּם.** וְיָעִיד עַל רֹעַ מִדַּת קְשִׁי הָעֹרֶף, כִּי אָמְנָם כְּשֶׁהִקְצַפְתֶּם אֶת ה׳ בְּחֹרֵב, לֹא הָיָה אוֹמֵר לְהַשְׁמִידְכֶם אֶלָּא בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁאַתֶּם עַם קְשֵׁה עֹרֶף, כַּאֲמָרוֹ יִתְבָּרַךְ ״רָאִיתִי אֶת הָעָם הַזֶּה וְהִנֵּה עַם קְשֵׁה עֹרֶף הוּא, הֶרֶף מִמֶּנִּי וְאַשְׁמִידֵם״ (דברים ט:יג-יד). וְזֶה כִּי אָמְנָם קְשִׁי הָעֹרֶף מֵסִיר אֶת תִּקְוַת הַתְּשׁוּבָה.


ובחורב הקצפתם, Moses underlines the serious nature of this negative characteristic known as “stiff-necked,” by reminding the people that even at Mount Sinai when G’d had already decided to wipe them out, (verse 14 our chapter) G’d had justified this by referring to their stiff-necked nature. He had not decreed death for them on account of a specific and horrific sin, but on account of their attitude which bode ill for the future, indicated that such a people was not capable of becoming true penitents.



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:15:1)


**וְהָהָר בֹּעֵר בָּאֵשׁ.** וּבְכֵן חֲטָאתֶם בְּעוֹד שֶׁהַמֶּלֶךְ בִּמְסִבּוֹ.


וההר בוער באש, so that you actually committed the sin (golden calf) while the “King” i.e. the presence of the Shechinah, was actually still in your immediate vicinity.



### Verse 22

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:22:1)


**וּבְתַבְעֵרָה וּבְמַסָּה.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁרְאִיתֶם שֶׁבְּהַקְצִיפְכֶם אֶת הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ נִשְׁבְּרוּ הַלּוּחוֹת וְהֻצְרַכְתִּי לְהִתְפַּלֵּל בַּעַדְכֶם בְּצוֹם אַרְבָּעִים יוֹם, חֲזַרְתֶּם לְהַקְצִיף בְּתַבְעֵרָה וּבְמַסָּה כְּשֶׁבִּכִּיתֶם בְּאָזְנָיו לְנַסּוֹת.


ובתבערה ובמסה, even though you have seen with your own eyes that because you had made G’d angry the Tablets He had made Himself were shattered, and I had to pray for you and fast for 40 consecutive days and nights, you repeatedly angered G’d again at the locations I just mentioned, as if putting G’d to the test each time.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 9:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/9:22:2)


**וּבְקִבְרוֹת הַתַּאֲוָה.** בִּשְׁאֵלַת הַבָּשָׂר.


ובקברת התאוה, when you demanded to eat meat.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:1:1)


**בָּעֵת הַהִיא אָמַר ה׳ אֵלַי פְּסָל לְךָ.** וְהִנֵּה עִם כָּל תְּפִלָּתִי הָיָה הַתִּקּוּן בִּלְתִּי שָׁלֵם, כִּי תְּמוּרַת הַלּוּחוֹת מַעֲשֵׂה אֱלֹהִים אָמַר אֵלַי פְּסָל לְךָ.


בעת ההיא אמר ה' אלי "פסל לך", in spite of all my prayers, the repair, the reconstitution of what had been damaged, proved to be incomplete,for the substitution for the first set of Tablets was not something made by G’d Himself, and this is why He commanded me: פסל לך, “carve it out by yourself!”



### Verse 6

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:6:1)


**וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל נָסְעוּ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁרָאוּ שֶׁתְּפִלַּת הַצַּדִּיק מְגִנָּה עַל דּוֹרוֹ וְשֶׁרָאוּי לְהִתְאוֹנֵן עַל מִיתָתוֹ, הִנֵּה קְצָתָם אוֹ רֻבָּם שֶׁהָיוּ רוֹעִים בַּמִּדְבָּר נָסְעוּ לְמוֹסֵרָה לִמְצֹא מַיִם וּמִרְעֶה לַצֹּאן, וּבְעוֹדָם שָׁם מֵת אַהֲרֹן וַיִּקָּבֵר וַיְכַהֵן אֶלְעָזָר, וְלֹא בָאוּ לְהִתְאוֹנֵן עַל הַמִּיתָה וְלֹא לְהִתְאַבֵּל עַל הַקְּבוּרָה, וְלֹא חָשְׁשוּ לְכַבֵּד אֶלְעָזָר שֶׁכִּהֵן תַּחְתָּיו, אֲבָל מִשָּׁם נָסְעוּ הַגֻּדְגֹּדָה לִרְעוֹת צֹאן.


ובני ישראל נסעו , even though they had seen that the prayer of a righteous person may prove a protection for the members of his generation so that it is most appropriate to mourn his death, in this instance we find that the Israelites in their search for water journeyed on without delay, not a word being said about their taking time out to mourn the passing of Aaron. This is the meaning of the construction: “there Aaron died, was buried…and Eleazar became High Priest in his stead.” They neither took time out to mourn Aaron nor did they celebrate the appointment of a new High Priest, a position which made that person the interlocutor on their behalf. Rather, משם נסעו הגדגודה, to graze their sheep;


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:6:2)


**וּמִן הַגֻּדְגֹּדָה יָטְבָתָה אֶרֶץ נַחֲלֵי מָיִם.** שֶׁכָּל אוֹתָם הַמְּקוֹמוֹת הַנִּזְכָּרִים עַתָּה הָיוּ אֶרֶץ נְחָלִים נְכוֹנִים לְמִרְעֵה הַצֹּאן, וְלֹא שָׂתוּ לִבָּם לְהֵיזֵק שֶׁקָּרָה בְּמוֹת הַצַּדִּיק, וְלֹא לִכְבוֹדוֹ וְלִכְבוֹד בְּנוֹ.


ומן הגדגודה יטבת, ארץ נחלי מים, for all the locations mentioned had water which was adequate for the needs of their livestock. Still, they paid no heed to the damage they had incurred by the death of a righteous man such as Aaron, neither to his honour, nor to the dishonour they had caused themselves by allowing the passing of a man of such caliber to go by unnoticed. [Numbers 20,28-29 reports Aaron’s death as well as the fact that the entire community of Israel bewailed his death for thirty days. Ed.]



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:8:1)


**בָּעֵת הַהִיא הִבְדִּיל.** וּמֵאַחַר שֶׁכְּבָר הִבְדִּיל הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ שֵׁבֶט מְיֻחָד לַעֲבוֹדַת הַמִּקְדָּש וְאֵין אַתֶּם רְאוּיִים לְהִשְׁתַּדֵּל בָּהּ, וְעִם זֶה לֹא הִשַּׂגְתִּי בִּתְפִלָּתִי רַק שֶׁלֹּא יַשְׁחִיתְךָ וְאָמַר לִי.


בעת ההיא הבדיל, after G’d had already set aside the tribe of Levi as the tribe to perform the holy service in the Temple so that you are not deemed worthy for this task, my prayer was not effective enough except to prevent G’d from destroying you, so that He said to me: “arise and make them journey, and be in front of the people!” (verse 11) The expression is used in the same sense as in Numbers 10,2)



### Verse 11

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:11:1)


**קוּם לֵךְ לְמַסַּע.** לְמַסֵּעַ, כְּמוֹ ״וּלְמַסַּע אֶת הַמַּחֲנוֹת״ (במדבר י:ב).


ויבואו ויירשו את הארץ אשר נשבעתי, if not for G’d’s oath you would not have merited to inherit it due to your excessive rebelliousness.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:11:2)


**וְיָבֹאוּ וְיִירְשׁוּ אֶת הָאָרֶץ אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁבַּעְתִּי.** כִּי לוּלֵא הַשְּׁבוּעָה לֹא הֱיִיתֶם זוֹכִים לָהּ לְרֹב מְרִיכֶם.



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:12:1)


**וְעַתָּה יִשְׂרָאֵל.** אִם כֵּן אַתָּה יִשְׂרָאֵל, הִשְׁתַּדֵּל עַתָּה לְתַקֵּן מְעֻוָּתְךָ מִכָּאן וָהָלְאָה, וְהִתְבּוֹנֵן מַה ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ שׁוֹאֵל מֵעִמָּךְ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ שׁוֹאֵל דָּבָר לְצָרְכּוֹ, כִּי אִם לְיִרְאָה. וְזֶה תַּעֲשֶׂה בְּהִתְבּוֹנְנְךָ בָּאֹפֶן שֶׁתֵּדַע גָּדְלוֹ.


ועתה ישראל, seeing that this is the situation as of now, it is up to you to try and repair the damage caused by your iniquities from here on in. First and foremost, try and be clear about what it is that the Lord asks of you, expects of you: מה ה' אלוקיך שואל מעמך, He does not ask these things because He is in need of them, seeing the whole earth and all of the celestial regions are all His. כי אם ליראה, you can do this by simply realising His greatness;


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:12:2)


**וּלְאַהֲבָה אוֹתוֹ.** וְזֶה תַּשִּׂיג בְּהִתְבּוֹנְנְךָ אֶל דַּרְכֵי טוּבוֹ. וְכָל זֶה הוּא שׁוֹאֵל לְטוֹב לְךָ, כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּזְכֶּה לְחַיֵּי עוֹלָם.


ולאהבה אותו, this will also be a natural result if only you reflect on all the goodness you have experienced at His hand. לטוב לך, all of this G’d asks only for your own good, so that you will qualify for eternal life in the hereafter.



### Verse 14

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:14:1)


**הֵן לַה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ הַשָּׁמַיִם.** וְיָעִיד עַל זֶה שֶׁהוּא מְבַקֵּשׁ לְטוֹב לָךְ, כִּי אָמְנָם הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ הֵם שֶׁלּוֹ, וְעִם הֱיוֹתָם נִכְבָּדִים בִּלְתִּי נִפְסָדִים.


הן לה' אלוקיך השמים...והארץ, the fact that He owns the entire universe is convincing proof that whatever He asks of you is only for your own good, not for His, seeing that He has everything. The phenomena in the heavens and on earth are indestructible, permanent, and if you observed that at times they performed out of character to their nature, this was only because I, G’d, made them perform so in your interest. However, G’d was fond of your forefathers



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:15:1)


**רַק בַּאֲבוֹתֶיךָ חָשַׁק.** שֶׁשִּׁנָּה אֶת טֶבַע אוֹתָם הַנִּכְבָּדִים בִּגְלַלְכֶם בִּזְכוּת אָבוֹת, וְאֵין זֶה בְּלִי סָפֵק אֶלָּא כְּדֵי לְהַשִּׂיג דָּבָר יוֹתֵר נִכְבָּד מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ, וְהוּא הָאָדָם הַשָּׁלֵם בִּהְיוֹתוֹ דּוֹמֶה לְבוֹרְאוֹ כְּפִי הָאֶפְשָׁר, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״בְּצַלְמֵנוּ כִּדְמוּתֵנוּ״ (בראשית א:כו).


רק באבותיך חשק, and this is why He performed miracles which interfered with natural law; these miracles were for your benefit, but were orchestrated only because of His fondness for them. Clearly, G’d’s purpose must have been that there should arise on earth someone even more perfect than any other creature, i.e. a perfect human being, one that realises his full potential, will resemble Me to the maximum extent that a creature can resemble its Creator, i.e. בצלמנו כדמותנו (Genesis 1,26).



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:16:1)


**וּמַלְתֶּם אֵת עָרְלַת לְבַבְכֶם.** אִם כֵּן רָאוּי שֶׁתָּסִירוּ אֶת עָרְלַת שִׂכְלְכֶם, וְהוּא שֶׁתִּתְבּוֹנְנוּ לְהָסִיר כָּל טָעוּת מוֹלִיד דֵּעוֹת כּוֹזְבוֹת.


ומלתם את ערלת לבבכם, therefore, it is appropriate that you remove the “foreskin,” prejudices with which your intelligence is afflicted, so that you will realise the errors you have made in your world outlook based on false premises.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:16:2)


**וְעׇרְפְּכֶם לֹא תַקְשׁוּ עוֹד.** וּבְסוּר קְשִׁי הָעֹרֶף הַמּוֹנֵעַ מִלִּפְנוֹת אֶל הָרָאוּי, תִּפְנוּ לְהַכִּיר אֶת בּוֹרַאֲכֶם וְתִרְאוּ כִּי רַע וּמָר לְעָזְבוֹ, וְזֶה שֶׁתִּתְבּוֹנְנוּ.


וערפכם לא תקשו עוד, so that you will no longer be stiff-necked, a condition which prevents you from truly recognising your Creator and how evil it is to turn your back on Him.



### Verse 17

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:1)


**כִּי ה׳ אֱלֹהֵיכֶם הוּא אֱלֹהֵי הָאֱלֹהִים.** הַנִּצְחִי עַל כָּל הַנִּצְחִיִּים הַנִּבְדָּלִים מֵחֹמֶר, וְזֶה שֶׁנִּצְחִיּוּת כֻּלָּם הוֹוָה מִנִּצְחִיּוּתוֹ.


Once you removed this “foreskin,” you will recognize that כי ה' אלוקיכם הוא אלוקי האלוקים, the eternal One Who will outlast anything as He is not made of any physical raw material so that He can outlast even materials that are not composites of other elements. [It is an axiom among philosophers of the author’s time that amalgams, phenomena which combine within them several elements, are by definition not durable, as just as these elements had to be fused to bring the final product into existence, this process will reverse itself in due cause, something we know as “dying.” Ed.]


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:2)


**וַאֲדוֹנֵי הָאֲדוֹנִים.** מַנְהִיג הַמַּנְהִיגִים, וְהֵם הַגַּלְגַּלִים וּמְנִיעֵיהֶם, שֶׁהַנְהָגַת כֻּלָּם מְכֻוֶּנֶת לְהַשִּׂיג תַּכְלִית כַּוָּנָתוֹ, כְּעִנְיַן הַמְּלָאכוֹת הַפְּרָטִיּוֹת בְּיַחַס אֶל הָרֹאשִׁיּוֹת.


ואדוני האדונים, the supreme leader; the “leaders” referred to are the celestial bodies, especially those that even to the naked eye appear as performing a recognisable orbit. The “leaders” are the ones perceived as guiding and dictating the respective orbits of these celestial bodies. They are performing like different parts of a clockwork, so that when each performs its assigned function, the whole, i.e. the clock, becomes a reliable instrument telling time. [my metaphor. Ed.]


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:3)


**הָאֵל הַגָּדוֹל.** שֶׁאֵין נִמְצָא שָׁוֶה לוֹ בְּמַדְרֵגַת מִין מְצִיאוּתוֹ.


הא-ל הגדול, the like of whom in greatness is not found among any creatures claiming greatness.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:4)


**הַגִּבּוֹר.** הַמְקַיֵּם כָּל הַמְּצִיאוּת בִּמְצִיאוּתוֹ, כְּאָמְרוֹ (נחמיה ט:ו) ״וְאַתָּה מְחַיֶּה אֶת כֻּלָּם״.


הגבור, the One Who, single-handedly, ensures the continued existence of everything, as per Nechemyah 9,6 ואתה מחיה את כולם, “and You keep all of them alive.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:5)


**וְהַנּוֹרָא.** מַשְׁגִּיחַ לְשַׁלֵּם וְלַעֲנוֹשׁ בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁרָאוּי לְיִרְאָה מִפָּנָיו.


והנורא, supervising His creatures’ activities in order to mete out reward and punishment. Seeing that this is so, it behooves us to be in constant awe of Him.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:6)


**אֲשֶׁר לֹא יִשָּׂא פָנִים.** לְבֵן פָּרִיץ מִפְּנֵי הָאָב הַצַּדִּיק, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא מַאֲרִיךְ אַף בִּזְכוּתוֹ שֶׁל אָב.


אשר לא ישא פנים, does not treat a deviant with leniency just because his father was righteous. This is so in spite of the fact that He confers benefits on the descendants of a righteous father for many generations to his descendants.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 10:17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/10:17:7)


**וְלֹא יִקַּח שֹׁחַד.** לֹא יָסִיר כְּלָל מֵעֹנֶשׁ הָעֲבֵרָה בִּשְׁבִיל זְכוּת מִצְוָה שֶׁעָשָה הַחוֹטֵא, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה שֶׁאֵין מִצְוָה מְכַבָּה עֲבֵרָה. וְכָל זֶה יוֹרֶה שֶׁלֹּא תִבְטְחוּ אִם תֶּחֶטְאוּ לְהִנָּצֵל בְּשׁוּם זְכוּת מִן הָעֹנֶשׁ כְּלָל, זוּלָתִי בַּתְְּשׁוּבָה הַשְּׁלֵמָה.


ולא יקח שוחד, He does not offset a merit against a demerit, a sin; or in the words of our sages: “a good deed performed does not extinguish the sin committed.” (Nachmanides Moses tells the people all this so that they should not reassure themselves that they can wipe the slate clean by performing good deeds after having committed sins and thus escape the retribution they deserve for the sins committed. The only way the slate can be wiped clean is by genuine repentance.

היא תהלתך, your glory consists in the privilege of being a servant of a Being Who rules the entire cosmos.

כי לא את בניכם, therefore, you who have become witnesses to G’d’s miracles for the generations by reason of the fact that you enter this land and conquer it.

וזרועו הנטויה, which is ever ready to smite those who will revert to their sinful ways.

**וַיְאַבְּדֵם ה׳ עַד הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה.** כִּי אָז טָבְעוּ שְָלִישֵׁי מִצְרַיִם וְחֵילָם בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁגַּם אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה אַחֲרֵי כֵן הָיוּ מַרְגִּישִׁים בַּאֲבֵדַת אוֹתָם הַגִּבּוֹרִים, שֶׁלֹּא קָמוּ עֲדַיִן מְמַלְאִים אֶת מְקוֹמָם.


ויאבדם ה' עד היום הזה, for then the choicest captains of the Egyptian cavalry were drowned with their armies in such a decisive manner that even now, forty years later, they have not recovered from this defeat. Until this day these military leaders of Egypt have not been replaced.

It is therefore up to you who have experienced all this with your own eyes to warn your children who have not been eyewitnesses to these events. Therefore,

**וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם אֶת כָּל הַמִּצְוָה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם.** מִצְוַת הָאֲבָנִים שֶׁתָּקִימוּ בַּיַּרְדֵּן וְשֶׁתִּקְחוּ מִתּוֹךְ הַיַּרְדֵּן וְשֶׁתִּכְתְּבוּ עֲלֵיהֶם אֶת הַתּוֹרָה, לִהְיוֹת לְדוֹרוֹת לְעֵדוּת מֵאִתְּכֶם שֶׁרְאִיתֶם.


ושמרתם את כל המצות אשר אנכי מצוך היום, the commandment to erect the stones in the Jordan as well as the stones you will take with you from the Jordan on which you will inscribe the Torah for the benefit of future generations so that the memory that these events really happened will be preserved.

**וּלְמַעַן תַּאֲרִיכוּ יָמִים עַל הָאֲדָמָה.** שֶׁאִם בְּנֵיכֶם לֹא יִשְׁמְרוּ אֶת הַמִּצְוֹת יִגְלוּ מִמֶּנָּה מְהֵרָה, קֹדֶם לְ״וְנוֹשַׁנְתֶּם בָּאָרֶץ״ (דברים ד:כה), כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵעִיד בְּאָמְרוֹ ״וְעָצַר אֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם״ (דברים יא:יז) ״וַאֲבַדְתֶּם מְהֵרָה״ (דברים יא:יז).


ולמען תאריכו ימים על האדמה, for if your children will not observe G’d’s commandments they will be exiled from this land before the warnings in the paragraph commencing with ונושנתם בארץ, “you will become well established in the land,” can ever become a reality (Deut.4,25).

### Verse 22

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 11:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22:1)


**כִּי אִם שָׁמוֹר תִּשְׁמְרוּן וְגוֹ׳ לְאַהֲבָה.** שֶׁיִּהְיֶה עִסְקְכֶם בַּתּוֹרָה כְּדֵי לְהַכִּיר חַסְדֵי הָאֵל יִתְעַלֶּה, אֲשֶׁר מִזֶּה תִּמָּשֵׁךְ הָאַהֲבָה.


כי אם שמור תשמרון לאהבה, the thrust of your preoccupation with Torah is to be that you show the acts of loving kindness G’d has performed, which in turn will ensure that you love Him.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 11:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22:2)


**וְלָלֶכֶת בְּכָל דְּרָכָיו.** לְהִתְנַהֵג בְּאוֹתָם הַדְּרָכִים אֲשֶׁר הוּא מְנַהֵג בָּם אֶת עוֹלָמוֹ, וְהֵם צְדָקָה וּמִשְׁפָּט.


ללכת בכל דרכיו, to emulate the way in which He governs His universe, i.e. application of justice and righteousness.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 11:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22:3)


**וּלְדָבְקָה בוֹ.** שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כָּל מַעֲשֵׂיכֶם מְכֻוָּנִים לַעֲשׂוֹת רְצוֹנוֹ, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״בְּכָל דְּרָכֶיךָ דָעֵהוּ״ (משלי ג:ו).


ולדבקה בו, all your actions should strive to carry out His will, as Proverbs 3,6 בכל דרכיך דעהו!



### Verse 23

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 11:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:23:1)


**וְהוֹרִישׁ.** יִתֵּן לָכֶם מָקוֹם לְהִתְפַּרְנֵס בּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא בְּצַעַר, לְמַעַן תּוּכְלוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת רְצוֹנוֹ.


והוריש, He will give you a chance to earn your livelihood easily so as to have time to serve Him.



### Verse 25

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 11:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:25:1)


**לֹא יִתְיַצֵּב אִישׁ בִּפְנֵיכֶם.** אֲפִילּוּ בְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ. חסלת פרשת עקב.


לא יתיצב איש, not even outside the land of Israel.



### Verse 26

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 11:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:26:1)


**רְאֵה.** הַבִּיטָה וּרְאֵה שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה עִנְיָנְךָ עַל אֹפֶן בֵּינוֹנִי כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא הַמִּנְהָג בִּשְׁאָר הָאֻמּוֹת, כִּי אָמְנָם **אָנֹכִי נֹתֵן לִפְנֵיכֶם הַיּוֹם בְּרָכָה וּקְלָלָה**, וְהֵם שְׁנֵי הַקְּצָווֹת. כִּי הַבְּרָכָה הִיא הַצְלָחָה יוֹתֵר מִן הַמַּסְפִּיק עַל צַד הַיּוֹתֵר טוֹב, וְהַקְּלָלָה הִיא מְאֵרָה מַחְסֶרֶת שֶׁלֹּא יוּשַּׂג הַמַּסְפִּיק, וּשְׁנֵיהֶם לִפְנֵיכֶם לְהַשִּׂיג כְּפִי מַה שֶׁתִּבְחֲרוּ.


ראה, pay good attention so that you will not be like the nations of the world who are always trying to find middle ground. Remember that אנכי נותן לפניכם היום ברכה וקללה, I present you this day with the choice of two extremes, opposites. The ברכה is an extreme in that it provides you with more than you need, whereas the קללה is another extreme making sure that you have less than your basic needs. You have the choice of both before you; all you have to do is make a choice.



### Verse 29

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 11:29:1

**וְנָתַתָּה אֶת הַבְּרָכָה.** תְּבָרֵךְ אֶת שׁוֹמְרֵי הַמִּצְוֹת.


ונתת את הברכה, bless the people who observe G’d’s commandments,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:29:2)


**וְאֶת הַקְּלָלָה.** לְעוֹבְרִים עֲלֵיהֶם.


ואת הקללה, and curse those who violate them deliberately.



### Verse 30

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 11:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:30:1)


**הֲלֹא הֵמָּה בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן.** בִּתְחִלַּת בּוֹאֲכֶם בָּאָרֶץ, כְּדֵי לְפַרְסֵם בִּתְחִלַּת כְּנִיסַתְכֶם שֶׁלֹּא תִהְיֶה יְשִׁיבַתְכֶם בָּהּ עַל אֹפֶן מַסְפִּיק, אֲבָל יִהְיֶה עַל אֹפֶן מֻצְלָח אוֹ עַל אֹפֶן מְקֻלָּל.


הלא המה בעבר הירדן, at the entrance to the land as soon as you cross the Jordan. This procedure is meant to demonstrate that your settling in this land will not be in an unsatisfactory, inadequate manner, but that it will be either completely successful or the reverse.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:1:1)


**אֵלֶּה הַחֻקִּים וְהַמִּשְׁפָּטִים אֲשֶׁר תִּשְׁמְרוּן וְגוֹ׳ לְרִשְׁתָּהּ כָּל הַיָּמִים.** וּמִכְלַל הַמִּצְוֹת הָאֵלֶּה הֵם שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁתִּשְׁמְרוּ אוֹתָן, אֲשֶׁר בְּקִיּוּמָן תִּירְשׁוּ הָאָרֶץ כָּל הַיָּמִים, שֶׁתְּאַבְּדוּ מְקוֹמוֹת כָּל עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה וְשֶׁלֹּא תַעֲשׂוּ כְמוֹתָם לָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ לְהַקְרִיב לְפָנָיו בְּכָל מָקוֹם, וְשֶׁיִּהְיֶה לָכֶם מָקוֹם מְיֻחָד לְהַקְרִיב לְפָנָיו, אוֹתוֹ הַמָּקוֹם אֲשֶׁר יִבְחַר ה׳ אֱלֹהֵיכֶם מִכָּל שִׁבְטֵיכֶם.


אלה החקים והמשפטים אשר תשמרון...-לרשתה כל הימים, observance of all the commandments listed forthwith is, among other things, a condition to ensure that you will remain ancestral owners of this land for all time. כל הימים, during all the time when you are busy wiping out locations dedicated to idolatry and you will not yourselves worship idols by becoming like them by offering your sacrifices wherever it pleases you.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5:1)


**לְשִׁכְנוֹ.** לִהְיוֹת שָׁם מִשְׁכַּן שְׁמוֹ, וְהוּא שִׁילֹה אוֹ בֵּית עוֹלָמִים.


המקום אשר יבחר ה' אלוקיכם מכל שבטיכם...לשכנו, the place where His Name will have a permanent residence, during the early period at Shiloh, later on in Jerusalem, Solomon’s Temple, better known as בית עולמים, the permanent Temple.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5:2)


**תִדְרְשׁוּ.** אֶל אוֹתוֹ הַמָּקוֹם הַנִּבְחָר תִּדְרְשׁוּ לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת וּלְהַקְרִיב, כְּעִנְיַן ״אֵלָיו גּוֹיִם יִדְרֹשׁוּ״ (ישעיהו יא:י).


תדרשו, you shall seek out this place to worship and to offer your sacrifices, as per Isaiah 11,10 אליו גויים ידרשו, “nations will seek Him out there.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 12:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5:3)


**וּבָאתָ שָּׁמָּה.** לֹא שֶׁיָּבֹא מִשְׁכַּן שְׁמוֹ אֲלֵיכֶם בְּכָל מָקוֹם כְּמִנְהַג עוֹבְדֵי עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה.


ובאת שמה, G’d will not come to you, as in the case of the idolaters, but you will come to Him.



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:7:1)


**לִפְנֵי ה׳ אֱלֹהֵיכֶם. וּשְׂמַחְתֶּם.** עִבְדוּ אֶת ה׳ בְּשִׂמְחָה, כָּרָאוּי לְכָל עוֹבֵד מֵאַהֲבָה.


(1) BEFORE ADONAI YOUR GOD, AND YOU SHALL FEAST. "Worship Adonai in gladness" (Ps. 100:2) as is appropriate for anyone who serves out of love.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 12:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:7:2)


**בְּכֹל מִשְׁלַח יֶדְכֶם.** כִּי אָז תַּצְלִיחוּ אֶת דַּרְכֵיכֶם וְאָז תַּשְׂכִּילוּ.


(2) IN ALL YOUR UNDERTAKINGS. For then you will succeed in all your ways and then you will prosper.

**וְאָמַרְתָּ אֹכְלָה בָשָׂר.** בִּלְתִּי שֶׁאֶצְטָרֵךְ לִטְרֹחַ לְהַעֲלוֹתוֹ לְבֵית הַבְּחִירָה.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 12:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:20:2)


**כִּי תְאַוֶּה נַפְשְׁךָ לֶאֱכֹל בָּשָׂר.** גַּם כֵּן כְּשֶׁתְּאַוֶּה לֶאֱכֹל אוֹתוֹ הַחֵלֶק הַנִּתָּן לַכֹּהֲנִים מִן הַזֶּבַח.
