למען ישמעו, in order that the wise men in the congregation would understand.

ולמען ילמדו, and in order that the less intelligent members of the people would learn it from them.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:12:3)

ויראו את ה' אלוקיכם, once they have understood the greatness of Torah they will automatically revere and be in awe of its author, G’d, whose supreme intelligence they will admire.

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:13:1)

ובניהם אשר לא ידעו, seeing that due to their tender age they are unable to formulate their questions if they have any.

ישמעו, either simply that their ears will hear sounds, or, if they are a little older, that they will hear words.

**וְלָמְדוּ.** יִשְׁאֲלוּ בְּגָדְלָם וְיִלְמְדוּ מִן הַמְּבִינִים.


ולמדו, they will ask adults and learn by listening intently to the answers they receive from people who do understand.

ועזבתים, to the various nations who will overpower them.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:17:2

**וְהִסְתַּרְתִּי פָנַי מֵהֶם.** אַעְלִים עֵינֵי חֶמְלָתִי מֵהֶם, כְּבִלְתִּי רוֹאֶה בְּצָרָתָם.


והסתרתי פני מהם, after they will have fallen under the control of the gentile nations who will abuse them and oppress them I will ignore my natural sense of pity, and make believe as if I were unaware of their plight.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:17:3)


**עַל כִּי אֵין אֱלֹהַי בְּקִרְבִּי מְצָאוּנִי הָרָעוֹת.** בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁסִּלֵּק שְׁכִינָתוֹ מִתּוֹכֵנוּ הָיוּ אֵלֶּה לָנוּ, וּבְחָשְׁבָם זֶה לֹא יִפְנוּ לְהִתְפַּלֵּל וְלֹא לָשׁוּב בִּתְשׁוּבָה.


על כי אין אלוקי בקרבי מצאוני הרעות, all of this has befallen me because G’d has abandoned me. Seeing that they feel abandoned, they will not bother to pray to Me, nor will they do teshuvah, considering such prayer futile, wasted.



### Verse 18

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:18:1)


**וְאָנֹכִי הַסְתֵּר אַסְתִּיר פָּנַי מֵהֶם.** לֹא כְּמוֹ שֶׁחָשְׁבוּ הֵם בְּאָמְרָם שֶׁאֵינִי בְּקִרְבָּם, כִּי אָמְנָם בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁיִּהְיוּ תִּהְיֶה שְׁכִינָתִי מְצוּיָה שָׁם, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (מגילה כ״ט) ״בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁגָּלוּ כוּ׳״, אֲבָל אַסְתִּיר פָּנַי מֵהַצִּילָם.


ואנכי הסתר אסתיר פני מהם, not as they thought that I was no longer in their midst; wherever they are My presence is with them, as our sages said in Megillah 29 “wherever the Jewish people have been exiled G’d’s presence accompanies them. However, it does not manifest itself by saving them from their oppressor.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:18:2)


**עַל כָּל הָרָעָה אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה.** הוּא לְעַצְמוֹ.


על כל הרעה אשר עשה, Israel had done harm to itself, not G’d.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:18:3)


**כִּי פָנָה אֶל אֱלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים.** כִּי בַּצָּרוֹת הַמּוֹצְאוֹת אוֹתָם עַל מָה שֶׁחָטְאוּ, הֵם אֵינָם פּוֹנִים אֵלַי לְעֶזְרָה בִּתְשׁוּבָה וּבִתְפִלָּה, אֲבָל פּוֹנִים לְהִמָּלֵט בְּאֶמְצָעוּת עוֹבְדֵי אֱלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים.


כי פנה אל אלוהים אחרים, while being beset by all of their problems, instead of turning to Me in prayer, supplication and remorse, they chose to pray to idols instead.



### Verse 21

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:21:1)


**כִּי יָדַעְתִּי אֶת יִצְרוֹ אֲשֶׁר הוּא עֹשֶׂה הַיּוֹם.** שֶׁאֵינָם מְצַפִּים לְהִכָּנֵס לָאָרֶץ כְּדֵי לְעָבְדֵנִי בָּהּ כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא הַמְכֻוָּן מֵאִתִּי, כַּאֲמָרוֹ ״וַיִּתֵּן לָהֶם אַרְצוֹת גּוֹיִם, בַּעֲבוּר יִשְׁמְרוּ חֻקָּיו״ (תהלים קה:מד־מה). אֲבָל מְצַפִּים אוֹתָהּ לְהַשְׂבִּיעַ נַפְשָׁם הַמִּתְאַוָּה, וְיִמָּשֵׁךְ מִזֶּה כָּל הָרַע שֶׁנִּבָּא, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וַיִּשְׁמַן יְשֻׁרוּן וַיִּבְעָט״ (דברים לב:טו).


כי ידעתי את יצרו אשר הוא עושה היום, for I, G’d, know its nature, its character; they do not look forward to taking over the land of the Canaanites in order to serve Me, as I wanted them to and hoped they would do. (compare Psalms 105,44-45 “He gave them the lands of nations; they inherited the wealth of peoples; in order to observe His statutes and His teachings, and observe His laws.”) Instead they look forward to gratify their own cravings as a result of which their excess wealth will be the cause of their decline and corruption, as per 32,15 “Yeshurun waxed fate and kicked, etc.”



### Verse 23

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:23:1)


**וַיְצַו אֶת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ.** הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ מִנָּה אָז אֶת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לְנָגִיד, כְּמוֹ ״צִוִּיתִי שׁוֹפְטִים״ (שמואל ב ז:יא) וּ״צִוְּךָ לְנָגִיד״ (שמואל א כה:ל) וְדוֹמֵיהֶם.


ויצו את יהושע, at that time G’d appointed Joshua to be the leader. The expression ויצו used here has been used elsewhere to describe someone’s appointment, such as in Samuel I 25,30 וצוך לנגיד, “He appointed you as leader,” or Samuel II 7,11 צויתי שופטים, “I appointed judges.” There are more such examples in Scripture.

על ספר עד תומם, together with the portions Haazinu and Vezot ha’brachah.



### Verse 26

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:26:1)


**וְהָיָה שָׁם בְּךָ לְעֵד.** יָעִיד שֶׁיָּדַעְתִּי שֶׁתַּעַזְבוּ אֶת תּוֹרַת הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ, שֶׁבִּשְׁבִיל זֶה הֻצְרַכְתִּי לָשִׂים סֵפֶר תּוֹרָה אֶחָד בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא יִכָּנֵס שָׁם אָדָם זוּלָתִי כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל אַחַת בַּשָּׁנָה, וְיָעִיד זֶה הַסֵּפֶר שֶׁכָּל מַה שֶּׁיִּמָּצֵא כָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר הַתּוֹרָה שֶׁיִּמָּצְאוּ בְּיַד צַדִּיקֵי הַדּוֹר, הֵן הֵן הַדְּבָרִים שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לוֹ לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי בְּלִי תּוֹסֶפֶת וְחִסָּרוֹן, וּבָזֶה לֹא יִוָּלֵד סָפֵק לָכֶם עֲלֵיהֶם. אָמְנָם הַסֵּפֶר שֶׁמָּצָא חִלְקִיָּהוּ (מלכים ב כב:ח) נִרְאֶה שֶׁהָיָה הַסֵּפֶר שֶׁנָּתַן מֹשֶׁה אֶל הַכֹּהֲנִים נֹשְׂאֵי אֲרוֹן בְּרִית ה׳ שֶׁהוּזְכַּר לְמַעְלָה (דברים לא:ט), שֶׁהָיָה פָּרָשַׁת הַמֶּלֶךְ בִּלְבָד, וּבוֹ כָּתַב יְהוֹשֻׁעַ הַבְּרִית שֶׁחִדֵּשׁ עִם יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּשְׂכַר שֶׁקִּבְּלוּ עֲלֵיהֶם בּוֹ לַעֲבֹד אֶת הָאֵל יִתְעַלֶּה בֶּאֱמֶת וּבְתָמִים, שֶׁהוּא בְּעִיּוּן וּבְמַעֲשֶׂה. וּכְשֶׁקָּרָא בוֹ יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ וְרָאָה שֶׁרָחֲקוּ מִכָּל זֶה, חָרַד וְדָרַשׁ אֶת ה׳ עַל זֹאת.


והיה שם בך לעד, the written Torah, reposing in the Holy Ark is witness that I have predicted and warned against your abandoning G’d and His Torah. This is why I arranged for this testimony to be preserved in a place accessible even to the High Priest only once a year on the Day of Atonement. This original copy of the written Torah will, if need be, prove that all that is written in all the Torah scrolls later and accessible to all the people all the time was a true copy of the Torah Moses had received from G’d at Mount Sinai. It will serve as proof that the Torah scrolls in circulation among the Torah scholars and the righteous people who commissioned copies for their private libraries, are all true to the original and do not contain additions or omissions.
 It would appear, however, that the Torah scroll found by the High Priest Chilkiyah (Kings II 22,8) was the very one which Moses had handed over to the priests who carried the Ark across the river Jordan which we referred to earlier. It contained only the passage dealing with the legislation pertaining to the king. It was this scroll in which Joshua wrote (added) the renewal of G’d’s covenant with the people which took place at Shechem. This was the record of Israel’s renewed undertaking to serve the Lord. Compare Kings II 22,13 where King Yoshiyahu refers to this scroll containing matters not contained in Moses’ original Torah scroll, as Joshua had written part of this scroll after the covenant’s renewal in Shechem. The King had become painfully aware how far the people had strayed from their solemn undertaking.

### Verse 29

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 31:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/31:29:1)


**כִּי יָדַעְתִּי.** אַגִּיד בְּאוֹתָהּ הַשִּׁירָה וְאָעִיד בָּכֶם שֶׁיָּדַעְתִּי שֶׁתַּעֲשׂוּ בְּאֹופֶן שֶׁתִּקְרֶה לָכֶם הָרָעָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תְיַחֲסוּ הֶעָתִיד אֶל הַמִּקְרֶה, אֲבָל תְּיַחֲסוּ אֶל מַה שֶׁהִשְׁחַתֶּם וְתִתְּנוּ לֵב לָשׁוּב. כְּעִנְיַן ״וָאַגִּיד לְךָ מֵאָז, בְּטֶרֶם תָּבוֹא הִשְׁמַעְתִּיךָ, פֶּן תֹּאמַר עָצְבִּי עָשָׂם״ (ישעיהו מח:ה).


כי ידעתי, in the course of that song (poem) I will reveal that I know how you will act so that the evil, if and when it befalls you, will not be perceived as “bad luck,” as coincidence, but can be traced back to my warnings. Once you realise and admit hat you had become guilty of a corrupt way of life, you will repent and turn to G’d in sincere remorse. Compare Isaiah 48,5 ואגיד לך מאז, בטרם תבוא השמעתיך, פן תאמר עצבי אשם, “Therefore I told you long beforehand, announced things to you before they happened, that you might not say: “my idol caused them.”

**אֶת דִּבְרֵי הַשִּׁירָה הַזֹּאת עַד תֻּמָּם.** עַד פָּרָשַׁת ״כִּי יָדִין״ וְ״כִי אֶשָּׂא״ (דברים לב:לו, מ), אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָם מִכְלַל דִּבְרֵי הָהַעֲדָה.


את דברי השירה הזאת עד תומם, including 32,36 כי ידין and כי אשא in 32,40, although those verses are not strictly speaking, part of Moses’ warning and testimony.

**יַעֲרֹף כַּמָּטָר לִקְחִי.** הִנֵּה תּוֹרָתִי הִיא תַּעֲרוֹף וְתָבֹא בְּשֶׁטֶף כַּמָּטָר לַמְּבִינִים הַמּוּכָנִים לְקַבֵּל מִבּוּעַ מְקוֹר חָכְמָה.


יערוף כמטר לקחי, here my teachings will pour down and flow without interruption like rain for those who understand and are prepared to receive it from the fountain, the source of wisdom.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:2:2)


**תִּזַּל כַּטַּל אִמְרָתִי.** וְנוֹתֶנֶת גַּם כֵּן כְּפִי הַנִּגְלֶה מִמֶּנָּה אֵיזוֹ יְדִיעָה לַהֶדְיוֹטוֹת, שְׁעִם הֱיוֹתָהּ מוּעֶטֶת הִיא טוֹבָה מְאֹד כַּטַּל, בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁהִיא כִּשְׂעִירִים עֲלֵי דֶשֶׁא, שֶׁהַמַּשְׂכִּילִים יַבִּיטוּ נִפְלָאוֹת מִמֶּנָּה.


תזל כטל אמרתי, and it too will contribute in accordance with the information which has been revealed to ordinary people. Even though these scraps of information are sparse, they are as useful to the recipients as is dew for the grass. The reason is that both in quantity and quality it is כשעירים עלי דשא, intelligent people can discern wonderful insights by listening to these words of mine; [וכרביבים עלי עשב, עשב being coarse, unrefined grass, it is a simile for coarse unrefined people. Ed.] even the most ordinary, uneducated people will acquire some knowledge by listening to what I have to say. It will help them to recognise and appreciate their Maker.

### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:3:1)


**כִּי שֵׁם ה׳ אֶקְרָא.** הִנֵּה קוֹרֵא שֵׁם ה׳ הוּא הַמִּתְפַּלֵּל, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״קָרָאתִי שִׁמְךָ ה׳ מִבּוֹר תַּחְתִּיּוֹת״ (איכה ג:נה), וְכֵן ״וּשְׁמוּאֵל בְּקֹרְאֵי שְׁמוֹ, קֹרְאִים אֶל ה׳ וְהוּא יַעֲנֵם״ (תהלים צט:ו). אָמַר: אִם כֵּן, כַּאֲשֶׁר אֶתְפַּלֵּל עַל קִבּוּץ גָּלֻיּוֹת בְּאָמְרִי ״כְּנֶשֶׁר יָעִיר קִנּוֹ״ (דברים לב:יא), וְעַל בִּיאַת הַמָּשִׁיחַ בְּאָמְרִי ״ה׳ בָּדָד יַנְחֶנּוּ״ (דברים לב:יב), וּבְסוֹף הַשִּׁירָה בְּאָמְרִי ״הַרְנִינוּ גוֹיִם עַמּוֹ״ (דברים לב:מג), אַתֶּם שֶׁיְּדַעְתֶּם גָדְלוֹ בְּמוֹפְתִים שִׂכְלִיִּים מְבֹאָרִים בְּתוֹרָתוֹ.


כי שם ה' אקרא, If this is as I said, then you Israel who have received these words of wisdom, will appreciate that the thrust of my address is a prayer, a prayer that all of us, including myself, will experience the ultimate redemption after the last exile. [Moses includes this in his preamble in order to make sure that his listeners will not fail to see the forest by concentrating too much on the individual trees. Ed.] Our author first proceeds to quote examples of such formulations as Moses uses here meaning prayer. We find in Lamentations 3,55 קראתי שמך ה' מבור תחתיות, ”I have called on Your Name from the depths of the Pit.” Clearly, Jeremiah refers to his prayer. We read in Psalms 99,6 concerning the trio of Moses, Aaron, and Samuel, that they were known as קוראי שמו והוא יענם, “that whenever they prayed to G’d He would respond to them favourably.”
 Moses therefore tells the people: “when I pray for the ingathering of the exiles I want G’d to orchestrate this so that it is like the eagle rousing his young and carrying them to a new destination on the back of his wings.” (verse 11) Moses continues, focusing in his prayer on the arrival of the Messiah by saying ה' בדד ינחנו, G’d will do this all on His own, without anyone’s assistance (verse 23). At the very end of his shirah, (verse 43) he exclaims הרנינו גויים עמו, which is equivalent to the instruction in our verse here of הבו גודל לאלוקינו, render glory to our G’d.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:3:2

**הָבוּ גֹדֶל לֵאלֹהֵינוּ.** אַל תְּיַחֲסוּ אֵלָיו הִשְׁתַּנּוּת עַל מָה שֶׁיְּסֻפַּר בַּשִּׁירָה מֵהָעֲתִידוֹת לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, וְשֶׁיֵּהָפֵךְ לָכֶם לְאוֹיֵב, כִּי יְדַעְתֶּם בְּמוֹפְתֵי תוֹרָתוֹ שֶׁהוּא.


(2) GIVE GLORY TO OUR GOD. Do not attribute to Him mutability regarding what is recounted in the poem about Israel's future and that He would become like an enemy to you, for you know, through the wonders in His Torah, that He is --"



### Verse 4

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:4:1)


**הַצּוּר.** בִּלְתִּי מִשְׁתַּנֶּה, לֹא קָצְרָה יָדוֹ מֵהוֹשִׁיעַ וְלֹא כָבְדָה אָזְנוֹ מִשְּׁמוֹעַ, וְגַם כֵּן לֹא תַיַּחֲסוּ אֵלָיו עָוֶל, אַחֲרֵי שֶׁיְּדַעְתֶּם בְּמוֹפְתִים כִּי תָמִים פָּעֳלוֹ בַּמְּצִיאוּת, עָלָיו אֵין לְהוֹסִיף וּמִמֶּנּוּ אֵין לִגְרֹעַ.


Do not relate to Him differently on account of what you hear in my song about Israel’s future. Do not come to the mistaken conclusion that G’d will ever change and that the experiences that will befall the Jewish people during the course of history, and which I am about to foreshadow, are evidence of His changing and possibly becoming the Jewish people’s enemy. You are very well aware through the variety of miracles He has performed on your behalf that הצור, He remains the Rock and His actions are always motivated by pure motives. This being so, if at times He does not appear to help you, save you, this is not due to a weakening on His part, neither is He hard of hearing so that He did not hear your pleas; never ascribe any injustice to Him;

כל דרכיו משפט, all of His paths are justice;

### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:5:1)


**שִׁחֵת לוֹ לֹא בָּנָיו מוּמָם דּוֹר עִקֵּשׁ וּפְתַלְתֹּל.** אֲבָל דּוֹר עִקֵּשׁ וּפְתַלְתֹּל לֹא בָּנָיו בִּתְמִימוּת, הִנֵּה מוּמָם בָּעֵגֶל. שִׁחֵת לָאֵל יִתְעַלֶּה אֶת הַמְכֻוָּן, כַּאֲמָרוֹ ״כִּי שִׁחֵת עַמְּךָ״ (שמות לב:ז). כִּי הוּא אָמְנָם כִּוֵּן לְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל וּלְקַדֵּשׁ שְׁמוֹ בְּעוֹלָמוֹ עַל יָדָם, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ לְמְאוֹרוֹת בַּמִּין הָאֱנוֹשִׁי לְהָבִין וּלְהוֹרוֹת, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כִּי לִי כָּל הָאָרֶץ וְאַתֶּם תִּהְיוּ לִי מַמְלֶכֶת כֹּהֲנִים״ (שמות יט:ה-ו). וְהֵם הִשְׁחִיתוּ כָּל זֶה בָּעֵגֶל.


שחת לו לא בניו מומם דור עקש ופתלתול, however, a twisted and perverse generation He does not view as His true children, such as when they made the golden calf. מומם, their blemish was not due to anything G’d had done Moses recalls that when G’d first told him about the people having become corrupt during his stay on the mountain that they were “your people” (Exodus 32,7), that although Moses, when accepting the conversion of the mixed multitude to Judaism had meant to sanctify the name of G’d, the fact was that instead of becoming a shining example to the world, these people had corrupted what would otherwise have been a kingdom of Priests. They had re-introduced idolatry among these people so recently weaned from it.

הלה' תגמלו זאת, after G’d had planned to make you superior over all other nations, is it indeed imaginable that you would respond by frustrating His intention by desecrating His name and to thereby prevent G’d from realising His declared intention when He created man that he should reflect the image of G’d? (Genesis 1,26)

ולא חכם, pursuing a policy while not considering its ultimate result.

**הֲלֹא הוּא אָבִיךָ קָנֶךָ.** לֹא אָב טִבְעִי הַנּוֹתֵן מְצִיאוּת מָה בְּמִקְרֶה, אֲבָל הוּא אָב רְצוֹנִי שֶׁנָּתַן לְךָ מְצִיאוּת לְמַעַן תִּהְיֶה קִנְיָנוֹ מוּכָן לְהַשִּׂיג בְּךָ חֶפְצוֹ וּסְגֻלָּתוֹ, וְזֶה כִּי הוּא עָשְׂךָ לְגוֹי, כִּי לֹא הָיִיתָ גּוֹי נֶחְשָׁב כְּלָל.


הלא הוא אביך קנך, He is not like a natural father who had no control over the type of son he had sired, but had to leave this to chance; G’d deliberately adopted the Jewish people giving them the kind of existence that would make Him proud to be called your “owner,” and made Him look forward to achieve His aim in creating mankind through you. Had He not made you into a nation you would have remained an insignificant tribe of sorts.

**וַיְכוֹנְנֶךָ.** נָתַן לְךָ הֲכָנוֹת שֶׁתִּהְיֶה מוּכָן בָּהֶם לִהְיוֹת לוֹ לְעַם סְגֻלָּה.


ויכוננך, He equipped you with the basic ingredients to enable you to become the precious nation He mentioned prior to the revelation at Mount Sinai.



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:7:1)


**זְכֹר יְמוֹת עוֹלָם.** אַחַר שֶׁהִשְׁלִים הַקְדָּמַת הַשִּׁירָה אֲשֶׁר בָּהּ הוֹדִיעַ שֶׁהַמְּכֻוָּן מִמֶּנּוּ הוּא לְהַגִּיד צִדְקַת הָאֵל יִתְעַלֶּה, שֶׁהוּא אֵל אֱמוּנָה לְהֵיטִיב לְעוֹשֵׂי רְצוֹנוֹ וּבֵרַךְ וְלֹא יְשִׁיבֶנָּה, וְאֵין עָוֶל בְּמִדַּת דִּינוֹ נֶגְדָּם. הוֹאִיל מֹשֶׁה בֵּאֵר זֶה בְּהַגָּדַת הֶעָבָר וְהֶעָתִיד, לְהוֹדִיעַ רִאשׁוֹנָה כַּמָּה הָיְתָה כַּוָּנַת הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ לְהַשִּׂיג זֶה הַתַּכְלִית בְּכָל הַמִּין הָאֱנוֹשִׁי בִּימוֹת עוֹלָם וּבִשְׁנוֹת דּוֹר וָדוֹר, וְאֵיךְ כְּשֶׁלֹּא עָלָה זֶה הִגְדִּיל ה׳ לַעֲשׂוֹת לְהַעֲלוֹת אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶל גֶּרֶם הַמַּעֲלוֹת, כַּאֲשֶׁר יַעֲשֶׂה בְּאַחֲרִית הַיָּמִים עִם הַשְּׂרִידִים אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָא. שֵׁנִית, שֶׁנָּתַן לָהֶם מָקוֹם נָכוֹן לְעָבְדוֹ בְּשִׂמְחָה וּבְטוּב לֵבָב מֵרֹב כֹּל, וְהֵמָּה מָרוּ וְשִׁלְּמוּ רָעָה תַּחַת טוֹבָה, וּמִי שֶׁהֶחֱטִיא כַּוָּנָתוֹ עִם כָּל אֵלֶּה, אֵין סָפֵק שֶׁהוּא רָאוּי לְעֹנֶשׁ נִמְרָץ. שְׁלִישִׁית, שֶׁבְּגֹדֶל עֲוֹנָם נָפְלוּ בְּמִכְמוֹרֵי רְשָׁעִים, וְהָיוּ רְאוּיִים לְכִלָּיוֹן חָרוּץ לוּלֵי חִלּוּל ה׳ שֶׁמָּנַע מִזֶּה. רְבִיעִית, הוֹדִיעַ סִבָּה אֲשֶׁר בִּגְלָלָהּ תִּהְיֶה לָהֶם גְּאֻלָּה בְּאַחֲרִית הַיָּמִים. חֲמִישִׁית, הוֹדִיעַ אֹפֶן הַגְּאֻלָּה וְרַחֲמֵי הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ עַל שְׁאֵרִית עַמּוֹ, וְהֵם חֶלְקֵי פָּרָשַׁת הַאֲזִינוּ שֶׁזָּכְרוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה וְנָתְנוּ בָּהֶם סִימָנִין הַזִּי״ו לָ״ךְ (ראש השנה לא). אָמַר אִם כֵּן: זְכֹר יְמוֹת עוֹלָם, וְתָבִין דַּרְכֵי טוּבוֹ וְכַמָּה כִּוֵּן לְהֵיטִיב לַמִּין הָאֱנוֹשִׁי בִּכְלָל, כְּשֶׁתִּזְכֹּר רִאשׁוֹנָה מַה שֶּׁהֵיטִיב לְאָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן בִּתְחִלַּת יְמֵי עוֹלָם וַיַּנִּיחֵהוּ בְּגַן עֵדֶן, וְהוּא קִלְקֵל עִנְיָנוֹ.


זכור ימות עולם, After concluding the introductory section of his poem, during the course of which he announced that it was his purpose to proclaim the perfect righteousness of the Lord G’d, Who has proven a loyal G’d showering goodness upon the people of Israel, and Who would not reverse a blessing once bestowed, Moses begins to discuss Israel’s past and future.
 First, Moses reminds the people that G’d had originally hoped to bestow a brilliant future for all of mankind, i.e. ימות עולם, He had hoped that history would develop in that direction. When this failed to happen, שנות דור ודור, during the years of one generation after another, G’d decided on His pilot project the Jewish people and He raised them to an elevated position among the nations. This is basically what He will do at the end of “history,” [history is perceived as a period of education for the people filling or making a brief appearance on the stage of history. Ed.]
 Second, G’d had provided the people with optimum conditions to serve Him joyfully while they enjoyed all the material advantages that life on earth has to offer. He gave them a beautiful country, one flowing with milk and honey, to do this in. Instead, they rebelled and repaid good with evil. There can be no question that the people responsible for frustrating G’d’s master plan deserve to be severely punished for this.
 Third, due to the serious nature of their sin they deserve to be wiped out completely. If it were not for the desecration of the Lord’s name that would be a by-product of the destruction of His chosen people, nothing would have prevented G’d from administering this punishment.
 Fourth, Moses reveals the reason why the redemption of the Jewish people will occur at the end of what we call “history,’ באחרית היים.
 Fifth, Moses elaborates somewhat on the nature of this redemption and the retribution G’d will wreak at that time on the other nations of the world. The nations will primarily be punished for what they did to the Jewish people throughout history. These then are the five sections into which the portion of Haazinu is divided, our sages having given us an easy way of reminding ourselves of this division by calling the portion הזי'ו ל'ך. (Rosh Hashanah 31) Moses therefore commences with the words: זכור ימות עולם, “study history and thereby learn to recognise how much G’d had endeavoured to shower good upon you, starting already with first man whom He transferred to Gan Eden, the most exclusive environment on earth.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:7:2)


**בִּינוּ שְׁנוֹת דּוֹר וָדוֹר.** וְתָבִין שֵׁנִית כַּמָּה הֵיטִיב לַדּוֹרוֹת וְהֵם הִשְׁחִיתוּ עַד הַמַּבּוּל. וְכֵן שְׁלִישִׁית מִן הַמַּבּוּל עַד הַפְּלָגָה וְהֵם קִלְקְלוּ.


בינו שנות דור ודור, you will then understand what amount of goodness G’d “squandered” on successive generations who did not deserve it. These generations of mankind, instead of appreciating it, did their best to ruin the universe until G’d was forced to put an end to them by bringing on the deluge.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:8:1)


**יַצֵּב גְּבוּלֹת עַמִּים.** וְהִצִּיב גְּבוּלוֹת עַמִּים בְּהַפְלָגָה וְלֹא אִבְּדָם לְגַמְרֵי כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ חַיָּבִים.


יצב גבולות עמים, after the tower of Babylon episode, when mankind had lived in a single community, G’d separated them, dividing them up, and established territorial boundaries for the various new nations. Instead of destroying the people who had engaged in building the tower as a challenge to G’d, He did not destroy them as He had the generation of the deluge, but limited their future scope of influence, by assigning only clearly marked territories within which they could be sovereign.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:8:2)


**לְמִסְפַּר בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** בִּשְׁבִיל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהָיוּ מְתֵי מִסְפָּר וְהָיוּ עֲתִידִים אָז לָצֵאת מֵאוֹתָם הָעַמִּים.


למספר בני ישראל. For the sake of the Children of Israel who were few in numbers, and who would eventually emerge from among these nations. Proof of all this is כי חלק ה' עמו, that G’d has no special claim on any nation other than the people of Israel. All the other nations worship alien deities. Moses explains that had it not been for the fact that G’d knew that the people of Israel would emerge eventually from among one of these nations, He would have wiped out all the people involved in the building of the tower.



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:9:1)


**כִּי חֵלֶק ה׳ עַמּוֹ.** כִּי אֵין לָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ חֵלֶק בְּמִין הָאֱנוֹשִׁי אֶלָּא עַמּוֹ, כִּי כָל שְׁאָר הָאֻמּוֹת הֵם בְּנֵי אֵל נֵכָר. וּבְכֵן הִתְבָּאֵר שֶׁלּוּלֵא עַמּוֹ שֶׁהָיָה עָתִיד אָז לָצֵאת מֵאוֹתָן הָאֻמּוֹת, הָיָה לוֹ לְאַבְּדָם בְּעִנְיַן הַפְּלָגָה.


יעקב חבל נחלתו, for he as well as his sons became servants of the supreme G’d. By contrast, whenever there was a pious and G’d fearing individual among the gentile nations, such piety and belief in G’d was never transmitted to and carried on by the next generation.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:9:2)


**יַעֲקֹב חֶבֶל נַחֲלָתוֹ.** שֶׁהוּא וּבָנָיו עֲבָדִים לָאֵל יִתְעַלֶּה, אֲבָל בְּאֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם אִם יִמָּצֵא בֵּינֵיהֶם אֵיזֶה חָסִיד, לֹא יִמָּשֵׁךְ זֶה לְבָנָיו.



### Verse 10

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:1)


**יִמְצָאֵהוּ בְּאֶרֶץ מִדְבָּר.** מָצָא לִבָּבוֹ שֶׁל עַמּוֹ שֶׁהוּא חֶלְקוֹ זֶה נֶאֱמָן לְפָנָיו בְּאֶרֶץ מִדְבָּר, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״לֶכְתֵּךְ אַחֲרַי בַּמִּדְבָּר״ (ירמיהו ב:ב).


ימצאהו בארץ מדבר, in the desert He found that the heart of this nation which He considered His only share among mankind was pure and loyal, as testified to in Jeremiah 2,2 “you followed Me into the desert.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:2)


**יְסֹבְבֶנְהוּ.** לְהַר סִינַי, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וְהִגְבַּלְתָּ אֶת הָעָם סָבִיב״ (שמות יט:יב).


יסובבנהו, to Mount Sinai, as mentioned in Exodus 19,12 when G’d had said to me: והגבלת את העם סביב, “and you will fence in the people around the Mountain.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:3)


**יְבוֹנְנֵהוּ.** כְּאָמְרוֹ (שמות כד:יב) וְהַתּוֹרָה וְהַמִּצְוָה אֲשֶׁר כָּתַבְתִּי לְהוֹרֹתָם.


יבוננהו, as G’d had said to Moses in Exodus 24,12 והתורה והמצוה אשר כתבתי להורותם, “and the Torah and the commandment which I have written in order to instruct them.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:4)


**יִצְּרֶנְהוּ.** מִשִּׁעְבּוּד מִצְרַיִם וּמִמַּלְאַךְ הַמָּוֶת, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (עירובין נ״ד): ״״חָרוּת עַל הַלּוּחוֹת״ – חֵרוּת מִמַּלְאַךְ הַמָּוֶת וּמִשִּׁעְבּוּד מַלְכֻיּוֹת״.


יצרנהו, He carefully engirded them from being enslaved to other nations and from being subject to the angel of death, as we have been taught by Eyruvin 54 that the words חרות על הלוחות, are best understood as חירות על הלוחות, freedom on the Tablets, i.e. acceptance of the Torah would result in the immortality of the person observing it totally.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:10:5)


**כְּאִישׁוֹן עֵינוֹ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּצַּר אֶת אִישׁוֹן עֵינוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם כְּשֶׁיְּצָרוֹ, שֶׁסִּבֵּב אֶת הָעַיִן בְּכָתְנוֹת עוֹר כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִפָּסֵד, עִם הֱיוֹתוֹ מוּכָן לְכָךְ.


כאישון עינו. Just as a man watches carefully over his eyeball after having tended it, so G’d watched over His people. G’d tried to surround the Israelites with layers of protective legislation to counter their natural tendency to be obstinate and “do their own thing.”



### Verse 11

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:1)


**כְּנֶשֶׁר יָעִיר קִנּוֹ.** יְהִי רָצוֹן שֶׁכְּמוֹ שֶׁחָשַׁב לַעֲשׂוֹת בְּסִינַי לוּלֵי חָטְאוּ בָּעֵגֶל, יַעֲשֶׂה לֶעָתִיד, וְקִבֵּל הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ תְּפִלָּתוֹ אָז כְּאָמְרוֹ ״אֶשְׁרְקָה לָהֶם וַאֲקַבְּצֵם כִּי פְדִיתִים״ (זכריה י:ח).


כנשר יעיר קנו, may it be His will that just as at Sinai it had been His intention to make you immortal, and He would have succeeded if the sin of the golden calf had not intervened, He will do so once more in the future, when Israel/Moses’ prayer in this respect will be granted. This is what Zecharyah 10,8 spoke about when he said: אשרקה להם ואקבצם כי פדיתים, “I will whistle to them and gather them, for I will redeem them.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:2)


**עַל גּוֹזָלָיו יְרַחֵף.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״הִנֵּה אֶשָּׂא אֶל גּוֹיִם יָדִי וְאֶל עַמִּים אָרִים נִסִּי וְהֵבִיאוּ בָנַיִךְ בְּחֹצֶן״ (ישעיהו מט:כב).


על גוזליו ירחף, as we read I Isaiah 49,22 הנה אשא על גוים ידי, ועל עמים ארים נסי, והביאו בניך בחצן, “I will raise My hand to nations, and lift up My ensign to peoples; and they shall bring your sons in their bosoms, etc.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:3)


**יִפְרֹשׂ כְּנָפָיו יִקָּחֵהוּ.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וְאַתֶּם תְּלֻקְּטוּ לְאַחַד אַחַד בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל״ (ישעיהו כז:יב).


יפרוש כנפיו יקחהו, as per Isaiah 27,12 ואתם תלוקטו לאחד אחד בני ישראל, “and you shall be picked up one by one, O Children of Israel.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:11:4)


**יִשָּׂאֵהוּ עַל אֶבְרָתוֹ.** כְּאָמְרוֹ (ישעיהו ס:ח) ״מִי אֵלֶּה כָּעָב תְּעוּפֶינָה״.


ישאהו על אברתו, as in Isaiah 60,8 מי אלה כעב תעופנה, “who are these who fly like a thick cloud?”



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:12:1)


**ה׳ בָּדָד יַנְחֶנּוּ.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כִּי אֶעֱשֶׂה כָלָה בְּכָל הַגּוֹיִם וְאוֹתְךָ לֹא אֶעֱשֶׂה כָלָה״ (ירמיהו מו:כח).


ה' בדד ינחנו, as per Jeremiah 46,28 כי אעשה כלה בכל הגויים...ואותך לא אעשה כלה, “though I will wipe out all the other nations …you I will not wipe out.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:12:2)


**וְאֵין עִמּוֹ אֵל נֵכָר.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כִּי אָז אֶהְפֹּךְ אֶל עַמִּים שָׂפָה בְרוּרָה לִקְרֹא כֻלָּם בְּשֵׁם ה׳״ (צפניה ג:ט), וּכְאָמְרוֹ ״אֱלֹהֵי כָל הָאָרֶץ יִקָּרֵא״ (ישעיהו נד:ה).


אין עמו אל נכר, as per Tzefaniah 3,9 and Isaiah 54,5, both of whom emphasise this exclusive nature of G’d.



### Verse 13

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:1)


**יַרְכִּיבֵהוּ עַל בָּמֳתֵי אָרֶץ.** וְזֶה יַעֲשֶׂה מֵאַחַר שֶׁלֹּא הִשִּׂיג חֶפְצוֹ בְּמַתַּן תּוֹרָה הַנִּזְכָּר, וִיבַקֵּשׁ זֶה שֵׁנִית בְּתֵת לָהֶם הָאָרֶץ (תהלים קה:מה) ״בַּעֲבוּר יִשְׁמְרוּ חֻקָּיו״, לְהַשְׁלִימָם בְּהַרְכִּיבָם עַל חֵלֶק מֵהָאָרֶץ נִשָּׂא בְּמָקוֹם וּבְמַעֲלָה, כְּאָמְרוֹ (יחזקאל כ:ו) ״צְבִי הִיא לְכָל הָאֲרָצוֹת״, וְזֶה בְּהִכָּנְסָם לָאָרֶץ עַתָּה.


ירכיבהו על במתי ארץ, this is something He has to do seeing that He did not achieve the desired result at the time He gave the Torah to the Jewish people at Mount Sinai. He will make a second attempt at achieving this as we know from Psalms 105,45 בעבור ישמרו חקיו, “in order that they may observe His statutes.” G’d gave them the land of nations in order that they keep His statutes.” Ezekiel 20,6 extols the land of Israel as ארץ צבי, the choicest of all the countries


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:13:2)


**וַיֹּאכַל תְּנוּבֹת שָׂדָי.** שֶׁלֹּא טָרְחוּ בָּם, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כְּרָמִים וְזֵיתִים אֲשֶׁר לֹא נְטַעְתֶּם אַתֶּם אֹכְלִים״ (יהושע כד:יג).


ויאכל תנובות שדי, Moses mentions only the eating, not referring to the labour in the fields leading up to this, eventually. He meant that it would not take any effort to reach the state of eating the produce the land has to offer. Compare Joshua 24,13 “vineyards and olive trees which you did not plant you are eating from.”



### Verse 14

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:14:1)


**וְדַם עֵנָב תִּשְׁתֶּה חָמֶר.** מִיץ הָעֲנָבִים הָיָה רָאוּי לִשְׁתִיָּה בִּלְתִּי רַב מְלָאכָה בּוֹ, וּבְכֵן הָיוּ מִתְפַּרְנְסִים שֶׁלֹּא בְּצַעַר. וְכָל זֶה עָשָׂה לָהֶם הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לָהֶם פְּנַאי לַעֲסוֹק בַּתּוֹרָה וּבַמִּצְוֹת.


ודם ענב תשתה חמר, grape juice was fit to drink without investing much toil beforehand. This is an example of people earning their livelihood easily. The reason why G’d had arranged things in such a fashion was so as to leave the people ample time to study Torah, and to enable them to fulfill the various commandments of the Torah which do consume time.



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:1)


**וַיִּשְׁמַן יְשֻׁרוּן וַיִּבְעָט.** וְהִנֵּה גַּם בַּעֲלֵי הָעִיּוּן שֶׁבָּהֶם הַנִּקְרָאִים יְשֻׁרוּן מִן ״אֲשׁוּרֶנּוּ וְלֹא קָרוֹב״ (במדבר כד:יז), עָשׂוּ כְּמוֹ הַבְּהֵמוֹת הַבּוֹעֲטוֹת בִּבְנֵי אָדָם שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים לָהֶם מָזוֹן.


וישמן ישורון ויבעט, however, even the most erudite of them, the ones known admiringly as Yeshurun, (based on Bileam’s prophecy of אשורנו ולא קרוב, in Numbers 24,17) acted as if they were animals, kicking the very people who give them food.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:2)


**שָׁמַנְתָּ עָבִיתָ כָּשִׂיתָ.** הִנֵּה אַתָּה יְשֻׁרוּן, קְהַל תּוֹפְשֵׂי הַתּוֹרָה וּבַעֲלֵי הָעִיּוּן, פָּנִיתָ אֶל הַתַּעֲנוּגִים הַגַּשְׁמִיִּים, וּבָזֶה עָבִיתָ מֵהָבִין דַּקּוּת הָאֱמֶת, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וְגַם אֵלֶּה בַּיַּיִן שָׁגוּ וּבַשֵּׁכָר תָּעוּ כֹּהֵן וְנָבִיא״ (ישעיהו כח:ז).


שמנת עבית כשית, you, the elite of the people, preferred to concentrate on the physical enjoyments offered by a life of leisure, and by doing so עבית, you became too gross to understand the finer points of the teachings of the Torah, the only truth. Isaiah 28,7 describes the conduct of the spiritual elite of the people when he writes: “but these are also so muddled by wine, and dazed by liquor, Priest and Prophet are muddled by wine;“


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:3)


**כָּשִׂיתָ.** כְּאָמְרוֹ (ישעיהו מד:יח) ״כִּי טַח מֵרְאוֹת עֵינֵיהֶם מֵהַשְׂכִּיל לִבּוֹתָם״.


כשית, compare what Isaiah 44,18 writes, “they have no wit or judgment, their eyes are besmeared and they see not;”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:4)


**וַיִּטֹּשׁ אֱלוֹהַּ עָשָׂהוּ.** וּלְפִיכָךְ נָטַש הֶהָמוֹן אֱלוֹהַּ עָשָׂהוּ.


ויטוש אלו-ה עושהו, so that, following the example of their elite, the people at large abandoned G’d the One Who had made them;


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:15:5)


**וַיְנַבֵּל צוּר יְשֻׁעָתוֹ.** בְּזֶהוּ כִּי מֵרָעָה אֶל רָעָה יָצָאוּ.


וינבל צור ישועתו, despising the Rock of its salvation, i.e. sliding from bad to worse in their lifestyles.



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:16:1)


**בְּתוֹעֵבוֹת יַכְעִיסוּהוּ.** לִגְרוֹם לַשְּׁכִינָה שֶׁתִּסְתַּלֵּק מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל.


בתועבות יכעיסוהו, causing the benevolent presence of G’d to forsake the people of Israel so that they would no longer enjoy an advantage over the gentile nations.



### Verse 17

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:1)


**לַשֵּׁדִים לֹא אֱלוֹהַּ.** שֶׁאֵינָם נִצְחִיִּים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה עַל הַשֵּׁדִים שֶׁהֵם מֵתִים כִּבְנֵי אָדָם (חגיגה טז).


לשדים לא אלוה, forces which are not enduring; our sages in Chagigah 16 already taught us that these demons known as שדים are as mortal as is man himself.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:17:2)


**אֱלֹהִים לֹא יְדָעוּם חֲדָשִׁים מִקָּרוֹב בָּאוּ.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ רוֹאִים אֵיזֶה מִין מִן הַנִּמְצָאוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יָדְעוּ מְצִיאוּתוֹ קוֹדֶם לָכֵן, הָיוּ חוֹשְׁבִים שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לוֹ אֵיזֶה מְסַבֵּב קַדְמוֹן מְיֻחָד אֵלָיו, וְהָיוּ עוֹבְדִים אוֹתוֹ.


אלוהים לא ידעום, חדשים מקרוב באו, whenever they discover a phenomenon not previously known to them they thought that this must be an ancient power and proceeded promptly to worship it.



### Verse 18

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:18:1)


**צוּר יְלָדְךָ תֶּשִׁי.** וְגַם אַתָּה יְשֻׁרוּן הַמְעֻיָּן, בִּפְנוֹתְךָ אֶל הַתַּעֲנוּגִים תְּנַשֶּׁה חָכְמָתְךָ וִידִיעָתְךָ בְּגָדְלוֹ.


צור ילדך תשי, even you, Yeshurun, the one blessed with erudition, once you turned your attention to the physical enjoyments of life on earth, undermined, weakened your wisdom and your knowledge about the greatness of G’d and His Torah.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:18:2)


**וַתִּשְׁכַּח אֵל מְחוֹלְלֶךָ.** שֶׁהֵטִיב עִמְּךָ, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״אָנֹכִי אָנֹכִי הוּא מְנַחֶמְכֶם וַתִּשְׁכַּח ה׳ עֹשֵׂךְ נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם כוּ׳״ (ישעיהו נא:יב-יג).


ותשכח א-ל מחוללך, Who had treated you so well. Compare Isaiah 51, 12-13 אנכי הוא מנחמכם...ותשכח ה' עושך נוטה שמים וארץ, “I, I am He Who comforts you!…You have forgotten the Lord your Maker, Who stretched the skies and made firm the earth.”



### Verse 19

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:19:1)


**וַיִּנְאָץ מִכַּעַס בָּנָיו וּבְנֹתָיו.** וְאַחַר שֶׁלֹּא הִשִּׂיג שְׁלֵמוּתָם בָּאָרֶץ, בָּחַר שְׁלִישִׁית לְצָרְפָם בַּגָּלוּת. וּבָזֶה וּמָאַס בְּכַעְסוֹ אֶת בָּנָיו וְאֶת בְּנוֹתָיו, לֹא רִחֵם עַל הַקְּטַנִּים וְלֹא עַל כְּבוֹד הַבָּנוֹת.


וינאץ מכעס בניו ובנותיו, after He had failed to make you attain your perfection while on your own land, He chose a third route, namely to refine your character while you were in exile; by doing this He appeared to despise His sons and His daughters, and did not display mercy on their children nor respect the dignity of their daughters.



### Verse 20

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:1)


**אַסְתִּירָה פָנַי מֵהֶם.** מִן הַבָּנִים וְהַבָּנוֹת.


אסתירה פני מהם, from the sons and daughters.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:2)


**אֶרְאֶה מָה אַחֲרִיתָם.** רוֹאֶה אֲנִי שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם תִּקְוַת תְּשׁוּבָה בָּאַחֲרִית.


אראה מה אחריתם, because I can foresee that there is no chance that they will become penitents.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:3)


**כִּי דוֹר תַּהְפֻּכֹת הֵמָּה.** הָפְכוּ לָהֶם לֵב אַחֵר נוֹטֶה מִדַּרְכֵי הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ.


כי דור תהפוכות המה for they have thoroughly turned their hearts away from the path of the living G’d;


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:20:4)


**בָּנִים לֹא אֵמֻן בָּם.** שֶׁלֹּא לָמְדוּ מֵאֲבוֹתָם דָּבָר אֲמִתִּי.


בנים לא אמון בם, completely faithless children who had not learned from their fathers any true values in life.



### Verse 21

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:1)


**הֵם קִנְאוּנִי בְלֹא אֵל.** הָאָבוֹת קִנְאוּנִי בְלֹא אֵל בְּבֵית רִאשׁוֹן.


הם קנאוני בלא אל, the generations during the first Temple aroused My jealousy because they practiced idolatry, i.e. they worshipped “non-gods,”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:2)


**כִּעֲסוּנִי בְּהַבְלֵיהֶם.** בְּבַיִת שֵׁנִי.


whereas the generations during the second Temple made Me angry with their reliance of various vain phenomena. As a fitting punishment


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:3)


**וַאֲנִי אַקְנִיאֵם בְּלֹא עָם.** בְּבַיִת רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהֶחֱרִיבוּהוּ כַּשְׂדִּים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּהֶם (ישעיהו כג:יג) ״הֵן אֶרֶץ כַּשְׂדִּים זֶה הָעָם לֹא הָיָה״.


אקניאם בלא עם, during the first Temple I will arouse their jealousy by the “non-people,” the Babylonians, destroying the Temple and taking them captive, (compare Isaiah 23,13 and how he describes the Chaldaens), whereas the second Temple will be destroyed through a thoroughly wicked nation


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:21:4)


**בְּגוֹי נָבָל אַכְעִיסֵם.** שֶׁהֶחֱרִיבָה אוֹתוֹ מַלְכוּת שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה הוֹגֶנֶת, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לָהֶם לֹא כְּתָב וְלֹא לָשׁוֹן (גיטין פ).


,בגוי נבל אכעיסם, possessing neither their own language nor alphabet. (Gittin 80).



### Verse 22

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:1)


**כִּי אֵשׁ קָדְחָה בְאַפִּי.** כִּי יִסַּרְתִּים כְּדֵי שֶׁיָּשׁוּבוּ. **וַתִּיקַד עַד שְׁאוֹל תַּחְתִּית.** הוֹסִיפוּ עַל חַטָּאתָם פֶּשַׁע בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁהִבְעִירוּ אֶת חֲמָתִי עַד שֶׁאוֹרִידֵם לִשְׁאוֹל תַּחְתִּית, כְּעִנְיַן אָחָז כְּאָמְרוֹ (דברי הימים ב כח:כב) ״וּבְעֵת הָצֵר לוֹ וַיּוֹסֶף לִמְעוֹל בַּה׳״.


כי אש קדחה באפי, for when I disciplined them in order to induce them to repent their sins this people lit a fire in My wrath ותיקד עד שאול תחתית, they heaped sin upon sin until My wrath burned to the very bottom of Sheol, (lowest layer of purgatory). In other words, their punishment would consist of being consigned to this lowest region of purgatory. (compare Chronicles II 28,22 describing Achaz adding sins as a result of his kingdom having been severely chastised by G’d.)


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:22:2)


**וַתְּלַהֵט מוֹסְדֵי הָרִים.** הֵסִירָה מֵהֶם כָּל רָאשֵׁי הָעָם, כְּאָמְרוֹ (ישעיהו ג:א-ג) ״מֵסִיר מִירוּשָׁלַיִם וּמִיהוּדָה מַשְׁעֵן וּמַשְׁעֵנָה גִּבּוֹר וְאִישׁ מִלְחָמָה יוֹעֵץ וְנָבִיא כוּ׳״.


ותלהט מוסדי הרים, it burned and consumed down to the foundations of the mountains. The “mountains” are a simile for the high-ranking personages, all of whom were the first ones to be exiled prior to the destruction of the first Temple. (compare prediction by Isaiah 3, 1-3)



### Verse 25

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:25:1)


**וּמֵחֲדָרִים אֵימָה גַּם בָּחוּר גַּם בְּתוּלָה.** וּמֵחֲדָרִים תְּשַׁכֵּל הָאֵימָה אֶת הַבַּחוּרִים וְהַבְּתוּלוֹת.


ומחדרים אימה, גם בחור גם בתולה, young men and women hiding in their houses will be made orphans.



### Verse 26

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:26:1)


**אָמַרְתִּי אַפְאֵיהֶם.** אַשְׁאִיר אֵיזוֹ פֵאָה מֵהֶם וְהַמּוֹתָר אֲכַלֵּם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֶעֱשֶׂה בְּאַחֲרִית הַיָּמִים, אַחֲרֵי שֶׁלֹּא הִשַּׂגְתִּי שְׁלֵמוּתָם לֹא בְּמַתַּן תּוֹרָה וְלֹא בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלֹא בַּגָּלוּת, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כִּי בְהַר צִיּוֹן וּבִירוּשָׁלַיִם תִּהְיֶה פְלֵיטָה כַּאֲשֶׁר אָמַר ה׳ וּבַשְּׂרִידִים אֲשֶׁר ה׳ קֹרֵא״ (יואל ג:ה).


אמרתי אפאיהם, I had said that I would leave a small “corner” (from פאה, the 1/60th of the farmer’s field to be left for the poor to help themselves to) of them survive whereas I would destroy the balance. This is what I will do at the end of history before the final redemption, seeing that I had been unable to achieve My purpose either through giving them the Torah, or through giving them the Land of Israel, or even through sending them into exile to give them a taste of what they had lost due to their obstinacy. (compare Yoel,3,5 warning the Jewish people of precisely this )



### Verse 27

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:1)


**לוּלֵי כַּעַס אוֹיֵב אָגוּר.** שֶׁמִּתּוֹךְ כַּעַס שֶׁיִּכְעֲסוּ עַל אוֹתָם הַשְּׂרִידִים, יֵשׁ לָחוּשׁ.


לולי כעס אויב אגור, that on account of the violent anti-semitism which the gentile nations would unleash against the small remnant of Jewish people so that in order to save themselves from further persecution they would פן ינכרו צרימו, attempt to divest themselves of their Judaism altogether, something the gentiles would hold out to them as a lifesaving gesture,


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:2)


**פֶּן יְנַכְּרוּ צָרֵימוֹ.** פֶּן יִמְשְׁכוּ אֶת לֵב הַשְּׂרִידִים לִהְיוֹת בְּנֵי נֵכָר כְּמוֹתָם.


פן יאמרו, and lest the gentiles would approach these remnants of the Jewish people saying to them


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:3)


**פֶּן יֹאמְרוּ.** לְאוֹתָם הַשְּׂרִידִים.


ידינו רמה, “we have prevailed, we have proven right with our religion, our faith,” and


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:4)


**יָדֵנוּ רָמָה.** לְהַכְחִיד אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל מִגּוֹי ״וְנִשְׁאַרְתֶּם מְתֵי מִסְפָּר״ (דברים כח:סב).


ולא ה' פעל זאת, “this was not what G’d did to you for not obeying Him,” so that you would still have hope to escape your fate by doing teshuvah. G’d scattered you to so many different places on earth that the total oblivion of the people through assimilation by inducements of the gentiles would not be likely. This was what Devorah meant when she said in her song in Judges 5,11 צדקות פרזונו בישראל, G‘d performed righteous deeds when He scattered Israel (instead of sending them to a single country into exile)


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:27:5)


**וְלֹא ה׳ פָּעַל כָּל זֹאת.** שֶׁתַּחְשְׁבוּ לְהִמָּלֵט עַל יָדוֹ. אֲבָל בִּהְיוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל רַבִּים וּנְפוֹצִים בַּאֲרָצוֹת רַבּוֹת, לֹא יַעֲלֶה עַל לֵב לְהִשְׁתַּדֵּל בָּזֶה, כְּאָמְרָם: מַאי דִּכְתִיב ״צִדְקוֹת פִּרְזוֹנוֹ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל״? צְדָקָה עָשָׂה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁפִּזְּרָם.



### Verse 28

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:28:1)


**כִּי גוֹי אֹבַד עֵצוֹת הֵמָּה.** הַבַּבְלִיִּים.


כי גוי אובד עצות המה, Moses is quoting the gentiles’ assessment of the Jewish people at that point.



### Verse 29

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:29:1)


**לוּ חָכְמוּ יַשְׂכִּילוּ זֹאת.** אֵיךְ נָפְלוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּיָדָם נֶגֶד הַטֶּבַע בַּעֲוֹנָם.


לו חכמו ישכילו זאת, how the Israelites could have fallen into their hands, something that is completely unnatural, could only have been due as punishment for their sins.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:29:2)


**יָבִינוּ לְאַחֲרִיתָם.** וּבָזֶה הָיוּ מְבִינִים מַה יִּהְיֶה בְּאַחֲרִיתָם בְּרֹב פִּשְׁעֵיהֶם.


יבינו לאחריהם, if they would only examine history they would soon realise what is in store for them for their sins if this is what has befallen Israel for its sins.



### Verse 30

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:30:1)


**אֵיכָה יִרְדֹּף אֶחָד אֶלֶף.** הָיָה לָהֶם לְהָבִין שֶׁאֵין זֶה בְּטֶבַע שֶׁאֶחָד יִרְדֹּף אֶלֶף.


איכה ירדוף אחד אלף, they should have realised that if they were able to pursue the Israelites, one against 1000, that this was not something natural.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:30:2)


**אִם לֹא כִּי צוּרָם מְכָרָם.** הוּא נִלְחַם בָּם וְהֵסִיר תָּקְפָּם, וּבְכֵן נָשְׁתָה גְּבוּרָתָם וְהָיוּ לְנָשִׁים.


אם לא כי צורם מכרם, He fought against them, neutralising their aggressiveness and turning them weak like women.



### Verse 31

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:1)


**כִּי לֹא כְצוּרֵנוּ צוּרָם.** וְזֶה קָרָה בְּלִי סָפֵק מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵין צוּרָם כְּמוֹ צוּרֵנוּ. כִּי אָמְנָם כָּל אֻמָּה מַצְלַחַת כָּל יְמֵי מֶמְשֶׁלֶת הַשַּׂר שֶׁלָּהּ אוֹ מַזָּלָהּ, וְלֹא יִהְיֶה הַשַּׂר לְעוֹלָם נֶהְפָּךְ לְאוֹיֵב עַמּוֹ. אֲבָל יִפְּלוּ בְּסֵר מֶמְשֶׁלֶת הַשַּׂר, וּבִמְשׁוֹל שַׂר אֻמָּה אַחֶרֶת, כְּפִי הַסֵּדֶר שֶׁנָּתַן בָּהֶם הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ. וּלְפִיכָךְ לֹא יֹאבְדוּ בְּאֹפֶן בִּלְתִּי טִבְעִי עַד שֶׁאֶחָד יִרְדֹּף אֶלֶף.


כי לא כצורנו צורם, no doubt what happened was due to the difference between their deities, and our G’d. Every nation enjoys a degree of success in its undertakings as long as its specific deity, i.e. agent appointed for them by G’d, is active, or its constellation is in the ascendancy in the sky. Although their specific deity will never become their adversary, they may fall within the power, the orbit of a competing deity, one that looks out for the interests of a different nation. This occurs in tandem with a master plan worked out by G’d for such historical developments. This is why when they “fall” this does not happen until one becomes so strong that it appears as if a single member of one nation can put a thousand of the competing nation to flight. [The author’s view of the collapse of empires appears based on the fact that when a major power was replaced by another this was not the result of a gigantic power struggle in which one nation proved stronger, but that the former world power had already declined in strength so much that they had become a pushover for the aggressor challenging them. Ed.]


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:2)


**וְאוֹיְבֵינוּ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכַּשְׂדִּים שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹיְבֵי ה׳ וְאוֹיְבֵי עַמּוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵעִיד בְּאָמְרוֹ ״לוּלֵי כַּעַס אוֹיֵב אָגוּר״ (דברים לב:כז), וּבְאָמְרוֹ ״מֵרֹאשׁ פַּרְעוֹת אוֹיֵב״ (דברים לב:מב).


ואויבינו, even though these nations which are enemies of the true G’d and therefore enemies of His chosen nation, as Moses mentioned already in verse 26 and again in verse 42 when he speaks of מראש פרעות אויב, “from the beginning of our people’s existence it had enemies.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:31:3)


**פְּלִילִים.** הֵם בַּעֲלֵי מַחֲשָׁבוֹת שִׂכְלִיּוֹת בַּמְּדִינוֹת, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וְהַאֲבַדְתִּי חֲכָמִים מֵאֱדוֹם״ (עובדיה א:ח), מִכְּלָל דְּאִיכָּא.


פלילים. philosophers of the various nations. Ovadiah 8 refers to them as והאבדתי חכמים מאדום, “I will wipe out the wise men from Edom.”



### Verse 32

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:1)


**כִּי מִגֶּפֶן סְדֹם גַּפְנָם.** וְהַטַּעַם שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם תְּבוּנָה בָּזֶה הָעִנְיָן הוּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵם מִתְגָּאִים בְּרֹב שַׁלְוָתָם וְנוֹתְנִים כָּל מַחֲשַׁבְתָּם לְהַשִּׂיג תַּעֲנוּגֵי גוּפָם בִּלְבַד כְּמוֹ אַנְשֵׁי סְדוֹם, וּלְהַשִּׂיג זֶה הָיוּ מוֹאֲסִים כָּל מִינֵי גְּמִילוּת חֲסָדִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵעִיד הַנָּבִיא בְּאָמְרוֹ (יחזקאל טז:מט) ״הִנֵּה זֶה הָיָה עֲוֹן סְדֹם אֲחוֹתֵךְ גָּאוֹן שִׂבְעַת לֶחֶם וְשַׁלְוַת הַשְׁקֵט הָיָה לָהּ וְלִבְנוֹתֶיהָ וְיַד עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן לֹא הֶחֱזִיקָה״.


כי מגפן סדום גפנם, the reason that in this matter they do not display their customary insights is that they boast about their secure and unthreatened existence, concerning themselves exclusively with physical gratifications just as had the people of Sodom. In order to attain their maximum gratification they completely eschewed all manner of caring for the less fortunate, as testified to by Ezekiel 16,49 when the prophet said הנה זה היה עוון סדום אחותך, גאון שבעת לחם ושלות השקט היה לה ולבנותיה ויד עני ואביון לא החזיקה. “only this was the sin of your sister Sodom; arrogance! She and her daughters had plenty of bread and untroubled tranquility. Yet she did not support the poor and the needy. “


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:2)


**עֲנָבֵמוֹ עִנְּבֵי רוֹשׁ.** וּמִזֶּה הַגֶּפֶן יִצְמְחוּ אֶצְלָם עִנְּבֵי רוֹש, שֶׁהֵם מַעֲשִׂים רָעִים וּמַזִּיקִים לַבְּרִיּוֹת, וְזֶה בְּהִשְׁתַּדְּלָם לָשִׂים עֵינֵיהֶם וְלִבָּם עַל בִּצְעָם וְעַל דַּם הַנָּקִי לִשְׁפֹּךְ.


ענבימו ענבי רוש, from this vine there sprouted forth for them grapes which are poison. This is a simile for the evil deeds committed by the Jewish people which backfired. The sins singled out are those of a social rather than a theological nature, as pointed out by Ezekiel.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:32:3)


**אַשְׁכְּלֹת מְרֹרֹת לָמוֹ.** וּבָזֶה יִקְנוּ דֵּעוֹת רָעוֹת וְכוֹזְבוֹת לְדַבֵּר עַל ה׳ תּוֹעֶה, וְיֹאמְרוּ ״לֹא יִרְאֶה יָהּ״ (תהלים צד:ז).


אשכלות מרורות למו, a bitter growth in their clusters; developing of erroneous philosophies, primarily justifying one’s conduct, rationalising it, is a natural corollary of such attitudes as self-centredness. First and foremost, they convince themselves that G’d does not ”see,” i.e. does not care, [that there is no personal G’d. They totally misinterpret G’d’s patience with the sinner. Ed.]



### Verse 33

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:33:1)


**חֲמַת תַּנִּינִם יֵינָם.** וְהִנֵּה בְּאוֹתָם אַשְׁכֹּלוֹת נִמְצָא יַיִן שֶׁהוּא חֲמַת תַּנִּינִים מְשֻׁמָּר בַּעֲנָבָיו, וְהוּא פֻּרְעָנוּת רָאוּי לָהֶם בְּמִדַּת הַדִּין וְנִשְׁמָר עַד שֶׁיּוֹצִיאֵהוּ הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ מֵאוֹתָם הָעֲנָבִים לְהַשְׁקוֹתָם, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״פּוּרָה דָּרַכְתִּי לְבַדִּי״ (ישעיהו סג:ג).


חמת תנינים יינם, their wine is the venom of asps; among the clusters mentioned in verse 32 there are also poisonous grapes, hidden and unidentifiable among the grapes. This disaster awaits them as a manifestation of the attribute of Justice they have richly deserved. Until they actually are extracting the juice from these grapes [this is all hyperbole, of course, Ed.] G’d did not convert His threat of retribution into something actual. Compare Isaiah 63,3, G’d saying that He alone squeezed out these grapes by treading on them.



### Verse 34

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:34:1)


**הֲלֹא הוּא כָּמוּס עִמָּדִי.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כִּי יוֹם נָקָם בְּלִבִּי״ (ישעיהו סג:ד).


הלא הוא כמוס עמדי, the meaning is similar to Isaiah 63,4 כיום נקם בלבי, “as the day of vengeance which I had kept hidden in my heart.” [the sins had to accumulate beyond reprieve before G’d carried out what had been at the back of His mind for a long time. Ed.]


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:34:2)


**חָתוּם בְּאוֹצְרוֹתָי.** כְּאָמְרוֹ (דניאל יב:ט) ״כִּי סְתוּמִים וַחֲתוּמִים הַדְּבָרִים עַד עֵת קֵץ״.


חתום באוצרותי, sealed away securely. Compare Daniel 12,9 כי סתומים וחתומים הדברים, “the matters are sealed and safely stored.”



### Verse 35

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:1)


**לִי נָקָם.** עָלַי מוּטָל לְהָשִׁיב נָקָם לְצָרָי.


לי נקם, it is up to Me to do the avenging from My enemies.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:2)


**וְשִׁלֵּם.** וְגַם כֵּן תַּשְׁלוּם מִדָּה כְּנֶגֶד מִדָּה.


ושלם, also the repayment tit for tat is My privilege and duty.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:35:3)


**לְעֵת תָּמוּט רַגְלָם.** כְּשֶׁתִּתְמַלֵּא סְאָתָם וְנָפְלוּ וְנִשְׁבָּרוּ.


לעת תמוט רגלם, the time when this will occur will be when they are already perceived as tottering.



### Verse 36

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:1)


**כִּי קָרוֹב יוֹם אֵידָם וְחָשׁ עֲתִידוֹת לָמוֹ כִּי יָדִין ה׳ עַמּוֹ.** כִּי אָמְנָם יִהְיֶה קָרוֹב יוֹם אֵיד הָאֻמּוֹת כַּאֲשֶׁר יָדִין ה׳ עַמּוֹ, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וּבָרוֹתִי מִכֶּם הַמּוֹרְדִים וְהַפּוֹשְׁעִים״ (יחזקאל כ:לח), כִּי אָז יֹאבְדוּ מְהֵרָה שְׁאָר הָאֻמּוֹת.


כי קרוב יום אידם וחש עתידות למו כי ידין ה' עמו, for the day when the nations will experience their judgment is near as soon as G’d will judge His own nation. We know this from Ezekiel 20,38 וברותי מכם המורדים והפושעים, “I will remove from amongst you the ones who rebel and are professional sinners.” Soon thereafter, the gentile nations will experience their own judgment.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:2)


**וְעַל עֲבָדָיו יִתְנֶחָם.** יִתְנַחֵם עַל הָרָעָה הָרְאוּיָה לְעַמּוֹ וִירַחֵם עֲלֵיהֶם בִּשְׁבִיל עֲבָדָיו שֶׁבְּתוֹכָם, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כֵּן אֶעֱשֶׂה לְמַעַן עֲבָדַי לְבִלְתִּי הַשְׁחִית הַכֹּל״ (ישעיהו סה:ח).


ועל עבדיו יתנחם, He will reconsider the evil times for which His people are due on account of His servants, the righteous among them, as we know from Isaiah 65,8 כן אעשה למען עבדי, לבלתי השחית הכל, “so will I do for the sake of My servants and not destroy everything.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:3)


**כִּי יִרְאֶה כִּי אָזְלַת יָד.** וְהַטַּעַם שֶׁיִּתְנַחֵם אָז עַל הָרָעָה הָרְאוּיָה לְעַמּוֹ וְשֶׁזֶּה יַעֲשֶׂה בִּשְׁבִיל עֲבָדָיו וְלֹא כֵן עָשָׂה כָּל יְמֵי הַגָּלוּת, הוּא כִּי אָז יִרְאֶה אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּתַחְתִּית הָעֹנִי וְלַחַץ הַגָּלוּת בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁאֵינָם יְכוֹלִים לִחְיוֹת עוֹד בּוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה הָעִנְיָן בְּמִצְרַיִם כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וַיַּרְא אֶת עָנְיֵנוּ וְאֶת עֲמָלֵנוּ וְאֶת לַחֲצֵנוּ״ (דברים כו:ז).


כי יראה כי אזלת יד, the reason why G’d does something for the people on account of His servants the righteous that He had not done during all the many years of the exile, is that He sees that this time they really had come to the end of heir rope; they had reached the kind of low that makes living on a hopeless undertaking. This is what had happened in Egypt immediately before the redemption, the Exodus. The Torah spells this out even in the recital by the farmer in Deut. 26,7 “He saw our plight, our misery and our oppression.” As a result, “He brought us to this place.” (the Land of Israel)


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:36:4)


**וְאֶפֶס עָצוּר וְעָזוּב.** וְיִרְאֶה שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם עוֹד מָמוֹן עָצוּר בַּבַּיִת וְלֹא עָזוּב בַּשָּׂדֶה.


ואפס עצור ועזוב, and none remains either imprisoned or roaming free. A reference to the people having no secret stash of cash hidden in their homes or buried in the field.



### Verse 37

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:37:1)


**וְאָמַר.** הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.


ואמר, at that point G’d will say to Israel


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:37:2)


**אֵי אֱלֹהֵימוֹ.** שֶׁל אֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם. כִּי אָז אָמְנָם יָסִיר שִׁלְטוֹן כָּל שָׂרֵי מַעְלָה וּמַטָּה, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״יִפְקֹד ה׳ עַל צְבָא הַמָּרוֹם בַּמָּרוֹם, וְעַל מַלְכֵי הָאֲדָמָה עַל הָאֲדָמָה״ (ישעיהו כד:כא).


אי אלוהימו, where is now this god of the gentile nations upon whom you have relied, spurning Me? This is the period when all of G’d’s agents who had been assigned as protective forces for the gentile nations will be simultaneously removed from their function. Compare Isaiah 24,21.



### Verse 38

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:38:1)


**יָקוּמוּ וְיַעְזְרֻכֶם.** וּכְמוֹ כֵן יֹאמַר הָאֵל לְיִשְׂרָאֵל: אַיֵּה הָאֻמּוֹת וּמְלָכֶיהָ שֶׁיָּקוּמוּ וְיַעְזְרוּכֶם מִן הַדִּין שֶׁלִּי, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֱיִיתֶם חוֹשְׁבִים לְהִתְגּוֹנֵן בָּהֶם בַּגָּלוּת.


יקומו ויעזרכם, “let them arise now and help you!” G’d will follow up by mockingly telling the Jewish people that now the time had come for them to appeal to the gentile nations whose lifestyles they had shared to help them against the judgment being visited upon them by the G’d, the One whom they had forsaken. They had assumed that by assimilating during their exile, these host nations as well as their gods would protect them.



### Verse 39

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:1)


**רְאוּ עַתָּה כִּי אֲנִי אֲנִי הוּא.** שֶׁהָיִיתִי מֵבִיא עֲלֵיכֶם פֻּרְעָנוּת.


ראו עתה כי אני אני הוא, realise now that it had been I Who had brought disaster in exile upon you!


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:2)


**וְאֵין אֱלֹהִים עִמָּדִי.** וְלֹא הָיָה זֶה מִכֹּחַ שָׂרֵי מַעְלָה וּצְבָא הַשָּׁמָיִם.


ואין אלוהים עמדי, I had not required the assistance of any other deity. Your misfortune was not due to any celestial forces appointed by Me.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:3)


**אֲנִי אָמִית וַאֲחַיֶּה.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וְהַעֲלֵיתִי אֶתְכֶם מִקִּבְרוֹתֵיכֶם״ (יחזקאל לז:יב).


אני אמית ואחיה, only I decree death and resurrection.(compare Ezekiel 37,12)


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:4)


**מָחַצְתִּי.** בְּמַכַּת נָע וָנָד, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וְהִכָּה ה׳ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל כַּאֲשֶׁר יָנוּד הַקָּנֶה בַּמָּיִם״ (מלכים א יד:טו).


מחצתי, I have smashed, by decreeing your unsettled condition, forcing you to move from one country to another without taking permanent root in any of them. (compare Kings I 14,15) where the prophet compares the existence of the Jewish people after G’d has chastised them as similar to that of a reed which is constantly moving.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:39:5)


**וַאֲנִי אֶרְפָּא.** כְּאָמְרוֹ (ישעיהו ל:כו) ״בְּיוֹם חֲבֹשׁ ה׳ אֶת שֶׁבֶר עַמּוֹ וּמַחַץ מַכָּתוֹ יִרְפָּא״.


ואני ארפא, and only I can bring healing, as described in Isaiah 30,26.



### Verse 40

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:40:1)


**כִּי אֶשָּׂא אֶל שָׁמַיִם יָדִי.** לְהִשָּׁבַע, כְּעִנְיַן ״וַיָּרֶם יְמִינוֹ וּשְׂמֹאלוֹ אֶל הַשָּׁמָיִם״ (דניאל יב:ז).


כי אשא אל שמים ידי, in order to swear an oath. This is a formulation similar to Daniel 12,7 וירם ימינו ושמאלו אל השמים,”he raised both his right hand and his left hand toward heaven.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:40:2)


**וָאָמַרְתִּי חַי אָנֹכִי לְעוֹלָם.** אֶשָּׁבַע שֶׁכְּמוֹ שֶׁאֲנִי חַי לְעוֹלָם, כְּעִנְיַן אָמְרוֹ ״וַיִּשָּׁבַע בְּחֵי עוֹלָם״ (דניאל יב:ז).


ואמרתי חי אנכי לעולם, I used the fact that I live forever as the guarantee that I can keep My oath. This too has a parallel in Daniel 12,7.



### Verse 41

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:1)


**אִם שַׁנּוֹתִי.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחִדַּדְתִּי נֶגֶד יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּימֵי הַחׇרְבָּן אֶת בְּרַק חַרְבִּי, לֹא פִי חַרְבִּי.


אם שנותי, even though I had sharpened the blade of My sword against the Jewish people during the time of their exile, את ברק חרבי, this was only the flashing appearance, not the פי החרב, the lethal part of the sword.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:2)


**וְתֹאחֵז בְּמִשְׁפָּט יָדִי.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאַחַר כָּךְ תֹּאחֵז בְּמִשְׁפָּט יָדִי נֶגְדָּם, כִּי יָדִין ה׳ עַמּוֹ. מִכָּל מָקוֹם.


ותאחז במשפט ידי, and even though I was compelled to deal with them according to the rule of law, for כי ידין ה' עמו, “G’d does judge His people,” this does not mean that their adversaries will go free, rather


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:3)


**אָשִׁיב נָקָם לְצָרָי.** הַנָּקָם שֶׁעָשׂוּ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, כַּאֲמָרוֹ ״יַעַן עֲשׂוֹת אֱדוֹם בִּנְקֹם נָקָם לְבֵית יְהוּדָה״ (יחזקאל כה:יב), אָשִׁיב לָהֶם אוֹתוֹ הַנָּקָם, כַּאֲמָרוֹ ״וְנָתַתִּי נִקְמָתִי בֶּאֱדוֹם בְּיַד עַמִּי יִשְׂרָאֵל״ (יחזקאל כה:יד).


אשיב נקם לצרי, I will wreak vengeance on My foes, reprisals for what they have done to Israel. Compare Ezekiel 25,12 and 25,14 on this subject in detail.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:41:4)


**וּלְמְשַׂנְאַי אֲשַׁלֵּם.** תַּשְׁלוּמֵי מִדָּה כְּנֶגֶד מִדָּה, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתָ כֵּן יֵעָשֶׂה לָּךְ, גְּמֻלְךָ יָשׁוּב בְּרֹאשֶׁךָ״ (עובדיה א:טו).


ולמשנאי אשלם, and I will pay back those who hate Me measure for measure, as spelled out in Ovadiah 15.



### Verse 42

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:1)


**אַשְׁכִּיר חִצַּי מִדָּם וְחַרְבִּי תֹּאכַל בָּשָׂר.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״כִּי בָאֵשׁ ה׳ נִשְׁפָּט וּבְחַרְבּוֹ אֶת כָּל בָּשָׂר״ (ישעיהו סו:טז).


אשכיר חצי מדם וחרבי תאכל בשר , similar to Isaiah 66,16 כי באש ה' נשפט ובחרבו את כל בשר, “for the Lord will contend with fire, with His sword against all flesh.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:42:2)


**מִדַּם חָלָל וְשִׁבְיָה.** כְּאָמְרוֹ ״וְרַבּוּ חַלְלֵי ה׳״ (ישעיהו כב:ב).


מדם חלל ושביה, as in the same verse in Isaiah ורבו חללי ה' “many will be the ones slain by the Lord.”



### Verse 43

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:1)


**הַרְנִינוּ גוֹיִם.** אַתֶּם חִצֵּי שה׳ וְחַרְבּוֹ, שֶׁיַּכִּירוּ כֻלָּם כִּי יָשָׁר ה׳ וְלֹא עַוְלָתָה בּוֹ, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״יִשְׂמְחוּ וִירַנְּנוּ לְאֻמִּים כִּי תִשְׁפֹּט עַמִּים מִישׁוֹר״ (תהלים סז:ה).


הרנינו גויים, “you who are the arrows of the Lord and His sword make the nations acclaim, sing aloud! So that they will realise that G’d is fair and does not commit any injustice. Compare Psalms 67,5 ישמחו וירננו לאומים כי תשפוט עמים מישור,”nations will exult and shout for joy, for You rule the peoples with equity”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:2)


**עַמּוֹ.** וְהַרְנִינוּ עַמּוֹ, שֶׁיִּתְּנוּ תּוֹדָה וְקוֹל זִמְרָה.


עמו, His people shall give thanks with shouts of joy and music, כי דם עבדיו יקום ונקם ישיב לצריו, for He does avenge the blood of His servants. G’d punishes the nations by avenging what they have done to the Jewish people.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:3)


**כִּי דַם עֲבָדָיו יִקּוֹם וְנָקָם יָשִׁיב לְצָרָיו.** וְרִנְנַת עַמּוֹ תִּהְיֶה עַל נִקְמַת דַּם עֲבָדָיו וְעַל נָקָם שֶׁיָּשִׁיב לְצָרָיו בִּשְׁבִיל מַה שֶּׁעָשׂוּ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל דֶּרֶךְ נְקָמָה.


וכפר אדמתו, for the soil of the Land of Israel having been desolate and barren while its people were in exile and for what had occurred on that soil in the interval.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:43:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:4)


**וְכִפֶּר אַדְמָתוֹ.** בָּהְשַׁמָּה מֵהֶם מִמַּה שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה בָּהּ.


עמו, His people will also have to atone for all the wrongs they did during the long years they had been in exile. The purpose of this dual atonement by both the soil and the people is to bring back the Shechinah, G’d’s benevolent presence to the Land of Israel, as envisioned in Isaiah 52,8 כי עין בעין יראו בשוב ה' ציון,”for every eye will see when G’d returns to Zion.”


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:43:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:43:5)


**עַמּוֹ.** וִיכַפֵּר גַּם כֵּן עַמּוֹ מִכָּל מַה שֶּעָשׂוּ מֵחִלּוּל ה׳ וְזוּלָתוֹ, בְּמַה שֶּסָּבְלוּ בַּגָּלוּת בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁיָּשִׁיב שְׁכִינָתוֹ בְּתוֹכָם, כַּאֲמָרוֹ ״יַחְדָּו יְרַנֵּנוּ כִּי עַיִן בְּעַיִן יִרְאוּ בְּשׁוּב ה׳ צִיּוֹן״ (ישעיהו נב:ח).



### Verse 46

###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:46:1)


**אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מֵעִיד בָּכֶם הַיּוֹם.** בְּשִׁירַת הַאֲזִינוּ שֶׁאֲנִי מֵעִיד וּמַתְרֶה בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁכְּמוֹ שֶׁנָּתַן לָהֶם הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ פַּרְנָסָה שֶׁלֹּא בְּצַעַר כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּעַבְדוּהוּ, כֵּן יַשְׁחִיתֵם בְּהַחְטִיאָם כַּוָּנָתוֹ.


אשר אנכי מעיד בכם היום, in the song of Haazinu which I use to make Israel be witness to my admonitions. The principal message is that just as while the Jewish people observe the commandments G’d arranges for their livelihood to be attained with ease and without much sweat, so, He will destroy them if they do not, because they thwart His plans for mankind as a whole.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:46:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:46:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:46:2)


**אֲשֶׁר תְּצַוּוּם אֶת בְּנֵיכֶם.** בְּסוֹף יְמֵיכֶם הַזְכִּירוּ בְּצַוַּאתְכֶם לִבְנֵיכֶם כָּל אֵלֶּה הַדְּבָרִים, וּתְצַוּוּם שֶׁיִּשְׁמְרוּ מְאֹד לְנַפְשָׁם פֶּן יַשְׁחִיתוּן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁצִּוָּה דָּוִד לִשְׁלֹמֹה בְּנוֹ.


אשר תצוום את בניכם, near the end of your respective lives command your children to observe the matters which I have spoken to you about. It is a matter of life and death for them.


###### Sforno on Deuteronomy 32:46:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:46:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/32:46:3)


**לִשְׁמֹר לַעֲשׂוֹת.** כְּדֵי שֶׁהַבָּנִים יִשְׁמְרוּ וְיַעֲשׂוּ.


לשמור לעשות, you have to observe all these matters in order that as their role model, your children will do so also.
